# Inventory: every feature and surface of today's app

Every feature and surface of StykAnimate as it is on `engine-rust` (`99c62189`), with where it lives today, its owner in
the native app ([architecture.md](architecture.md)), the test that checks it today, its screen ID and its status. The
native port is done when every row reaches **same** or **better**; nothing is dropped silently. A row that truly cannot
carry over says why and names its replacement, which is built.

Built on 2026-11-06 by twelve readers, one per area, from the code and the docs (the code wins where they disagree; each
area's port notes say where), then checked mechanically: every interface module, desktop file, IPC channel, feature doc,
command and stylesheet in the repository is referenced at least once. Appendices A to C are generated from the code.

**Status:** `todo` (not ported), `wip` (a chunk is on it), `same` (works the same, checked), `better` (works better,
checked; the row says how), `replaced` (cannot carry over: the reason and the replacement are in the row).

**Screen IDs** name every visible surface for the screen catalogue and the screenshot comparison: `styk-cli` renders any of
them headless, and each area's "Screens and states to capture" table lists their states and how to reach them.

| Appendix C: every stylesheet | CSS | 93 |

## Shell, window, top band, menus, docking today, shared UI and the design system (SH)

Scope: everything that frames the editor and is shared by every workspace: the boot path and start-up queue, the top band (mark, application menus, project tabs, workspace switch, history, Save, Export, session buttons, window controls), the tool rail, the stage header (crumb, tool options bar, message slot, view toggles, zoom), the dock host (layout model, solver, presets, strips, sheets, floating panels, splitters, tab drag, keyboard moves), the overlay kit (menus, popovers, dialogs, toasts, tooltip, command palette, command registry, quick actions, tool options, toolbar overflow), the app's own prompts, error presentation, and the whole design system (tokens, fonts, icons, component styles, transitions). Main files: `src/app.mjs` (shell parts only), `src/application-menu.mjs`, `src/app-dialogs.mjs`, `src/studio-shell.mjs`, `src/shell/*`, `src/ui/*`, `src/project-tabs.mjs`, `src/project-tabs-host.mjs`, `src/theme.mjs`, `src/ui-scale.mjs`, `src/icons.mjs`, `src/styles/**`. Contract docs: `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md`, `docs/PROJECT-TABS.md`, `docs/design/` (experience.md, visual-system.md, components.md, ui-architecture.md, DECISIONS.md, function-ledger.md), `docs/hotkeys.md`, `docs/APP-START.md`. Where code and docs disagree the code is stated in Port notes. Features owned by other areas (the inspector, timeline, stage gestures, Home, Settings dialog body, export dialog, library, VFX, drawing, build, touch, tutorials, updates and privacy dialogs) get no row here; they appear only as cross-references in Port notes.

### Screens and states to capture

| Screen ID | States (default, hover, pressed, focus, selected, disabled, empty, loading, error, open/closed, light and dark when it differs) | How to reach it (document, steps, window size if it matters) |
|---|---|---|
| shell.boot | Prepaint dark window before settings; light and dark first paint; busy "Preparing canvas and effects" task; Ready | Launch the desktop app with a fresh profile and capture the first frames (1280x800) |
| shell.band | Animate default, Draw, Build; Home showing (Home pressed, no workspace pressed); undo/redo disabled and enabled; edited project; Properties sheet button visible; light and dark; roomy (above 800 tall) and compact chrome | Open `tdd2` or any sample; windows 1440x900 and 1280x720 |
| shell.band.narrow | Each fold step: tight, name, save, testrig, export2, switchtight, icons, dot, redo, primary; menubar folded; minimal tier | Resize from 1500 down to 600 wide with 3 projects open; also in a session with Done and Cancel |
| shell.band.session | Build (Test rig, Done, Export quiet), movieclip (Cancel, Done), camera (Done); each at full width and folded | Enter Build via the Build switch; create a movieclip from selection; open the camera from the timeline |
| shell.window-controls | Default, hover on minimize/maximize, hover on close (danger-soft), pressed, 36 px folded; macOS traffic-light padding | Desktop build on Windows and macOS |
| menu.app.mark | Closed, open, with open-projects submenu (2+ projects), submenu open, hover, focus, disabled rows (New window, Open recent unavailable), light and dark | Click the StykAnimate mark |
| menu.app.file / edit / view / joint / animation / help | Open; item hover; item focus; submenu open (Import, Panels, Duplicate, Flip, Arrange, Movieclip); disabled rows (no figure, no joint); checked toggles in View; danger rows; desktop-only Help groups | Open each through the mark menu submenu, or Alt-equivalent focus path |
| menu.project-tab | Front project tab menu; resting project tab menu; Close saved projects disabled | Right-click a tab with 2+ projects open |
| shell.tabs | 1 tab, 2, 6, 12 (limit); front, resting, hover (Close replaces dot), focus, edited dot, saving, saved flash, narrow tab (no close, name dropped), picture vs placeholder icon, rtl | Open several samples; edit one; save one |
| shell.tabs.drag | Dragged tab dimmed; drop line before and after | Drag a tab across another |
| shell.tabs.switcher | Board with 2, 6 and 12 cards, selected card, Edited vs Saved labels, with pictures and without | Hold Ctrl and press Tab with 2+ projects |
| shell.workspace-switch | Animate, Draw, Build pressed; hover; focus; label folded to icons; dots hidden; Home showing | Click each workspace |
| shell.rail | Animate tools, Draw tools, Build tools (two columns with quick actions), active tool, hover, focus, tick visible, compact size (44), moved to right side, rail-order buttons (Animate) | Switch workspaces; move rail via Layout popover |
| menu.add | Animate (with project figures, with more than 10, empty project), Draw (New drawing layer first), Examples submenu | Click rail + in Animate; the tick in Draw |
| menu.rail-variants | Select pixels shapes, Shapes kinds, current one checked | Draw workspace; right-click or long-press the tool |
| shell.context-strip | Select, Pose, Tweener, Pan ("view only"), Build shared definition, Draw exposure, with Return to animation | Under the stage in each workspace |
| dialog.insert | Default, search filled, empty project, examples expanded | Add menu > All in this project |
| menu.properties-more | Animate figure, Build part, Build figure, disabled rows, movieclip selected | Inspector header ellipsis |
| shell.stage-head | Crumb with scene size and background chip, rec background chips, tool options bar, message over crumb, message dropped, lesson strip, light and dark, 40 and 44 px header | Each workspace at 1440 and 900 wide |
| shell.stage-head.crumb | Scene, session path (Scene > clip), size folded, name folded | Enter a movieclip session |
| shell.stage-head.options | Tool options per tool: toggles, select, field, stepper, colours, recent, segmented; folded items; More options trigger | Select Brush, Line, Shape tools; narrow the stage |
| popover.tool-options-more | Open with moved controls | Narrow the stage until options fold |
| shell.stage-head.view | Joints, Paths, Grid split, Effects pressed and unpressed, Build set, labels folded under 820 | Switch workspaces; narrow the stage |
| menu.stage-view | Touch folded View menu with checks | Touch interface, phone width |
| menu.zoom | Open, items hover | Click the zoom readout |
| toast.message | Info, success, warning, error, with action (Undo), dismissible, with timer paused on hover, waiting queue, in header centre, dropped line, bottom centre, inside a dialog | Trigger "Finish or cancel the polygon first", a save error, an Undo toast |
| toast.mode | Pick-mode instruction with Cancel and Esc tip | Start Connect joints in Build |
| toast.task | Slow work with indeterminate bar; with determinate progress; with Cancel | Large import or export |
| toast.error | Persistent failed save (stays), timed failure (8 s), user-action warning | Save to a read-only folder |
| toast.recovery | Single copy offer, multiple copies, buttons hover | Restart after a forced close with unsaved work |
| dialog.recovery | Select closed, open, discard in progress, empty after discards, error | Restore > Manage copies |
| shell.dock | Wide default (rail, stage, right dock, bottom timeline); left dock in Build; stacked islands; light and dark | Window 1440x900 each workspace |
| shell.dock.tiers | Wide, regular, narrow, compact, minimal widths; tall, short, low heights | Resize to 1500, 1300, 1100, 780, 620 wide; 700, 600, 500 tall |
| shell.dock.tabs | Properties, VFX, Library tabs; selected, hover, focus, drag source dimmed | Right dock |
| shell.dock.strip | Left strip, right strip, bottom (timeline) strip; icon hover with tooltip; open sheet | Fold a panel from the tab menu |
| shell.dock.sheet | Properties sheet at compact width; strip sheet; timeline body sheet at low tier; focus return | Resize below 800 wide; below 568 tall |
| shell.dock.float | Floating VFX or Library over the stage; two floating panels | Tab menu > Float |
| shell.dock.splitter | Default, hover, drag, focus ring, at min and at max | Drag the inspector edge; Home resets |
| shell.dock.drop | Drop targets: Dock left, Dock right, Add as a tab, Float here, Stack above/below, Move tools | Drag a tab out of its place |
| shell.dock.keyboard-move | Preview drop zone with place name | Focus a panel, Ctrl+Alt+M |
| menu.panel-tab | Docked, strip, floating variants | Right-click a tab or strip icon |
| popover.layout | Animate presets (Animate, Timing, Review, Focus), Draw, Build; saved presets present; dock-map cards; selects open; at limit of 8 | View > Layout... or Ctrl+Alt+L |
| menu.layout-preset | Rename, Update, Delete (danger) | Right-click a saved preset card |
| shell.timeline.sheet | Timeline header only (low tier) with body sheet open; Expand tall state | Window below 568 tall; Expand button |
| palette.command | Empty (Recent, Commands), query with highlights, no results, unavailable rows with reasons, Go to frame, Settings and In this project groups, compact full-window with close | Ctrl+P |
| dialog.generic | sm, md, lg; busy; error inline; meta text; danger action; pointer-opened (no ring) and keyboard-opened (ring) | Any dialog below |

### Port notes

- Code versus docs: `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` says the light `theme-color` is `#f5f6f9` and that Inter Tight is unused. The code writes `#eceff4` (the light window field, `--mist-175`) in `src/theme.mjs`, `src/theme-bootstrap.js` and `index.html`, and `#0a0b0f` dark; and Inter Tight is the display face of every button, heading, title and numeral (`--type-button`, `--type-heading`, `--type-title`, `--type-numeral`). Use the code.
- `docs/PROJECT-TABS.md` describes `name` and `dot` as fold steps; the code has 15 steps (`BAND_FOLDS`) and `band.css` says `state`, `name8` and `savelabel` free nothing and are skipped by the measurement. The band's menubar is never visible in any tier (`display: none`); all menus reach the user through the mark menu. A native app should either build a real menubar or keep this folded model, but the six menus and their rows are the contract.
- `index.html` hard-codes `title="Undo - Ctrl+Z"` style strings on the band buttons (and a translated twin); at run time the tooltip kit adopts them and takes the shortcut from the live registry, so a rebinding changes the tooltip. Port the registry-driven form only.
- Menus are authored as `[action, icon, label, shortcut, disabled]` tuples and converted; the shortcut text in the tuple is a fallback. The shown text is the user's first binding. Delete-like rows are red and last.
- Hotkeys never run while any overlay of kind dialog/menu/palette/keyboard-scoped popover is open, or any `dialog[open]` exists, and held keys are released at that moment. A native app needs the same single gate rather than per-widget checks.
- Timings to preserve: tooltip 500 ms (0 ms within 300 ms of the last hide); toast 4 s info/success, 6 s warning, errors sticky, failures 8 s, pause on hover or focus; task toast delay 400 ms; menu typeahead 700 ms; rail long press 450 ms with 6 px slop; tab drag slop 4 px; saved flash 2.2 s; layout save debounce 200 ms; resting-project recovery 1.5 s quiet then retries 1 s / 15 s; playback readout 5 Hz; first-recovery GPU retry delays (stage area).
- Band folds must follow the measured widths in order and must never depend on the window size inside CSS; a language change or a session's extra buttons re-measure. The native band needs the same measure-then-fold data flow (or an equivalent priority layout), and the stored widths trick is a start-up optimisation only.
- Layout data is part of the user's files in practice: `workspace-preferences.json` must stay readable by the Electron versions (v1 mirror keys `studioVersion`, `right`, `timeline`, unknown keys preserved, read-only for a newer version). If the native app shares the same preferences file during the transition, keep the schema v2 round trip exactly (`tests/layout-schema.test.mjs`, `tests/layout-store.test.mjs`).
- Layout tiers are measured on the `#app` content box after UI scale, not on the screen, so tiers never oscillate; the solver is pure and should move to `styk-core::layout` unchanged, with its tests.
- Only the project in front is live. Resting tabs keep document, history and view state but no workers or caches; the 12-project limit and the 48 MB history budget per resting project are part of the memory contract.
- The window-close question (Save all / Cancel / Discard all, stops at the first cancelled save) is in the main process and documents area; cross-reference rather than rebuild here.
- Recovery: copies are per project (`recovery-<token>.styk`); at start every copy no open project owns is offered; the legacy single `recovery.styk` is still read.
- Start-up measurements in `docs/APP-START.md` (ready around 1.1 s, stage at 1.8 to 2.6 s, GPU process contention, bundle overlay, source maps for crash frames) are Electron and Chromium specific. What carries over: do not block the interface thread, queue main-process pushes until listeners are ready, and show a quiet busy message only after 400 ms.
- Compare-before-write and surface-scoped updates exist because DOM writes forced style recalculation during playback (transport readout 5 Hz, quiet surfaces during playback). In a retained-mode or immediate-mode native UI the equivalent is not rebuilding view models for hidden or unchanged surfaces while playing.
- Fonts: the font subsets omit arrows and symbols; the UI draws them as icons. A native text stack must still render all scripts the locales cover (Arabic, Hebrew, Chinese use system fonts today) and right-to-left layout.
- Cross-references (owned elsewhere, no rows here): Home page and its band button behaviours beyond the pressed state (home area); Settings dialog body, scale and theme controls (settings area); inspector panels and their sections (inspector area); timeline header, ruler and layers (timeline area); stage gestures, context menus on the canvas and overlays (stage area); export dialog, the Export button's dialog (export area); touch layout, modifier dock, long-press helper and sheets on touch (`src/touch/`, touch area); tutorials strip content (tutorials area); updates UI and privacy dialogs (updates and support areas); the support centre (`src/support-center.css`, copied byte for byte to the website; never edit it for the editor).
- Test gaps: no test exists today for the window controls, rail ticks and long press, the Layout popover's selects, tab drag-to-dock, panel tab menu, the Add menu contents, the context strip, the Performance dialog or the Joint and Animation menu rows; the native port should add tests for these as it ports them.

## The Animate stage (ST)

Scope: everything a person sees and does on the Animate canvas. The tool rail (Animate tools), the stage header (location crumb, Joints / Motion paths / Grid / Effects toggles, zoom cluster, mode messages, tool options), the viewport with its scene canvas and 2D overlay canvas, selection and handles, posing (pose, bend, stretch, isolate, handle edit, figure transform, Alt-drag duplicate, nudging), joining and disconnecting figures, per-node IK controls, the 3D (spatial) rig controls and rotation gizmo, wheel/pinch/pan/zoom, the grid and guides, the canvas context menu, the segment-values dialog, the touch modifier dock and the stage HUD. Main files: `src/app.mjs` (pointer handlers around lines 2296-3200, `setTool`, `pick`, `overlayRender`, `loop`, `zoom`, `fit`, `actions`), `src/canvas-interactions.mjs`, `src/canvas-handle-overlay.mjs`, `src/canvas-guides.mjs`, `src/canvas-pinch.mjs`, `src/canvas-wheel-zoom.mjs`, `src/canvas-pose-input.mjs`, `src/editor-selection.mjs`, `src/spatial-controls.mjs`, `src/spatial-gizmo.mjs`, `src/ik*.mjs`, `src/shell/rail.mjs`, `src/shell/stage-head.mjs`, `src/engine/engine-stage.mjs`. Contract docs: `docs/SCENE-EDITING.md`, `docs/FIGURE-JOINING.md`, `docs/inverse-kinematics.md`, `docs/SPATIAL-MESHES.md`, `docs/ROTATING-CUBE.md`, `docs/figure-stacking-and-property-keys.md`, `docs/segment-values.md`, `docs/hotkeys.md`. Editing itself (pose solving, IK solving, mesh skinning, bending, translation, undo) is the Rust engine (`styk-edit`, `styk-eval`); the stage owns input interpretation, hit-testing, gestures, overlays and view state, which move to `styk-core::stage` and `styk-ui::stage`. The Build workspace (builder tools, rig tool, polygon, chain, fills), Draw workspace, camera gizmos, motion-path trails, onion skins, VFX gizmos, timeline, inspector panel layout, colour picker and settings dialog are owned by other areas and appear here only as cross-references in Port notes.

### Screens and states to capture

| Screen ID | States | How to reach it |
|---|---|---|
| animate.stage | default (artboard with figures, handles on), empty document, fitted versus zoomed versus panned, hover over a handle (cursor), dragging cursor, playing (overlay hidden), previewing, dark and light theme, 1280x720 and a narrow 700 px window | Animate workspace; open any sample with several figures |
| animate.rail | default Animate tools, each tool active (Select, Tweener, Pan), hover tooltips (name plus shortcut), focus ring, Add menu open | Animate workspace, left rail |
| animate.rail.tweener-menu | closed, open with each type checked (4 types), keyboard focus on an item | Right-click the Tweener tool or press its arrow |
| animate.stage.tweener-hud | hidden, visible at 50% (Breakdown), negative value in Blend to neighbor, per-type colour | Tweener tool, drag horizontally on the canvas |
| animate.stage.hint | hint text per tool (hidden in the current layout, captured for the accessible description) | Switch tools |
| animate.stage-head.crumb | Scene 960 x 540, narrow (size/name folded), inside a movieclip or camera session (scope path, Done), background chip swatch | Stage header; enter a movieclip |
| animate.stage-head.view | Joints on/off, Motion paths on/off, Effects on/off, label folded to glyph under 820 px, View menu fold (phone/touch) | Stage header, right side |
| animate.stage-head.grid | grid off, on, guides only, split-button hover | Stage header Grid split |
| animate.stage-head.zoom | percent label at several zoom levels (5.5%, 100%, 400%), chevron pressed | Stage header zoom cluster |
| animate.stage-head.mode | join-mode pill with Cancel, tweener pill, drop-to-own-line placement, pill over the crumb | Select a figure, press J |
| animate.stage-head.note | "Preview detail reduced" note | Play a dense scene with quality lowered |
| menu.zoom | open with Fit selection enabled and disabled | Click the zoom percentage |
| popover.guides | closed, open default, guides locked (fields disabled), flipped above, narrow window | Click the Grid chevron |
| animate.stage.grid | grid at 40 px, doubled spacing at low zoom, opacity extremes | Grid on, zoom out |
| animate.stage.guides | vertical and horizontal guides, label near an edge flipped, dragging, locked | Guides on |
| animate.stage.handles | unselected blue, selected red, origin orange, joined white/grey, static grey, IK blue outline, connector diamond, join-mode colours, auto-size versus fixed, outline off, detail controls shown/hidden | A figure with joins, IK and static joints |
| animate.stage.marquee | marquee while dragging (Origins and Bounds), toggle variant | Drag on empty canvas |
| animate.stage.overlay | figure-transform pivot with dashed guide, duplicate preview during Alt-drag, multi-figure selection | Alt-drag a joint; Alt-drag a root |
| animate.stage.join-mode | join target handles (red/orange/white), toast on a miss | Select a figure, press J |
| animate.stage.gizmo3d | three-axis rings with disc, single hinge ring, labelled joint, hover and drag | 3D rig example, Rotate mode |
| animate.stage.hud | fps badge good/low/pending, Preparing, Playing, Preview | Press Space to play |
| animate.stage.drop | drop hint visible | Drag a file over the window |
| animate.stage.error | device-lost panel with Retry, reconnecting | Simulate device loss |
| animate.stage.modifier-dock | collapsed, expanded, a latched modifier, Depth offered, per-tool sets | Touch mode on, select a figure |
| animate.inspector.ik | IK off, IK on with options, chain stepper limits | Select a node, Properties |
| animate.inspector.rig3d | each section (Pose/Rotate mode, pad, Body shortcuts, Limb, Mechanism, Exact angles, Perspective, Shape, Choices) | 3D rig example selected |
| animate.inspector.colors | figure palette rows, node palette, mixed | Figure selected, Properties |
| menu.canvas-context | empty-canvas menu, figure menu, joint menu, Paste as submenu, disabled Join/Disconnect | Right-click the canvas |
| menu.figure-more | open | Canvas menu, More figure actions |
| dialog.segment-values | default, relative on, Handle Edit caption, clipboard fallback visible, error, Apply disabled (invalid), scrubbing | Hover a node and press I |
| toast.stage | join hints, branch selected, connected figures selected, motion paths on/off | Trigger from stage actions |
| settings.canvas | stage-related rows (zoom, smooth zoom, box select, handles, radius slider) | Settings, Canvas tab |

### Shortcuts

| Keys | Action | Context | Where (code) |
|---|---|---|---|
| V | Select tool | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`tool-select`) |
| P | Pose tool (alias of Select and pose) | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`tool-pose`) |
| H | Pan tool | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`tool-hand`) |
| (unassigned) | Edit shared rig tool, Polygon tool, Toggle joint isolation, Toggle Rectangle selects, Toggle Edit together | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` |
| Hold R | Handle Edit (drag handle positions) | global, hold | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`handle-edit-hold`) |
| Hold CapsLock | Isolate joint | global, hold | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`isolate-hold`) |
| Hold D | Pose 3D parts in depth | global, hold | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`depth-hold`) |
| J | Join / Unjoin figure; again cancels | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`join-figure`) |
| Shift+J | Show / hide joints | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`toggle-handles`) |
| M | Show / hide motion paths | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`toggle-motion-paths`) |
| Ctrl+' (Mod+Quote) | Show / hide grid | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`toggle-grid`) |
| Ctrl+H | Show / hide unselected handles | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`toggle-unselected-handles`) |
| = and Shift+= | Zoom in (x1.2) | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`zoom-in`) |
| - | Zoom out | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`zoom-out`) |
| Shift+1 | Fit canvas | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`zoom-reset`) |
| Shift+2 | Fit selection | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`zoom-selection`) |
| Shift+H / Shift+V | Flip figure(s) horizontally / vertically | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`mirror`, `mirror-vertical`) |
| I | Segment values for the hovered or selected node (camera handle: focus camera field) | canvas | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`segment-values`) |
| Arrow keys | Nudge hovered or selected handle 1 px; held keys stay one gesture | canvas | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`handle-*`) |
| Shift+Arrow | Align handle with neighbours | canvas | `src/app.mjs` (`keyboardHandle`) |
| Alt+Arrow (Shift = 10 px) | Move selection | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`move-*`) |
| Ctrl+Left / Ctrl+Right | Select previous / next figure | canvas | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`previous-figure`, `next-figure`) |
| L / Shift+L | Locate (match) handle at previous / next pose | hover over a handle | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`locate-*`) |
| Ctrl+A | Select all figures (frames when the timeline is focused) | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`select-all`) |
| Ctrl+D | Duplicate selection | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`duplicate`) |
| Delete / Backspace | Delete selection | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`delete`) |
| Ctrl+C / Ctrl+X / Ctrl+V / Ctrl+Shift+V | Copy, cut, paste, paste in place | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` |
| Escape | Cancel gesture, join mode, pick mode, placement | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`cancel`); `src/app.mjs` (`cancelKeyboardCommand`) |
| Space (hold) + drag | Pan; a tap plays/pauses | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`play-pan`) |
| Ctrl+Space + drag | Zoom about the press (right or up zooms in) | stage, pointer | `src/app.mjs` (pointerdown) |
| Shift+Space + drag | Rotate the view (Draw only), snaps upright within 3 degrees | Draw | `src/app.mjs` |
| Mouse wheel | Pan (default) or zoom (setting) | stage | `src/app.mjs` (wheel handler) |
| Ctrl/Cmd + wheel | Zoom about the pointer | stage | same |
| Shift + wheel | Horizontal pan | stage | same |
| Middle-button drag | Pan | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Click / Shift+click / Ctrl+click | Select / toggle figure | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Double-click node / body | Select branch / connected figures (Shift adds) | stage | `src/app.mjs` (`dblclick`) |
| Right-drag a joint | Bend joint | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Shift+right press | Immediate bend, no context menu | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Ctrl/Cmd+drag joint | Stretch joint length | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Shift+drag joint | Alternate bend solution | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Ctrl/Cmd+drag origin | Axis-locked move | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Shift+drag origin | Move with pinned IK targets | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Alt+drag joint | Rotate and scale figure(s) about the picked origin (Ctrl locks scale, Shift locks rotation) | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Alt+drag origin | Duplicate and move | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Drag on empty canvas (+Shift/Ctrl) | Marquee select (toggle) | stage | `src/app.mjs` |
| Shift while dragging pad or gizmo | Precision | spatial controls | `src/spatial-controls.mjs`, `src/spatial-gizmo.mjs` |
| Arrow keys on a guide label (Shift 10) | Nudge guide | guide focused | `src/canvas-guides.mjs` |
| F6 / Ctrl+F6 | Move focus between areas (stage included) | global | `docs/hotkeys.md` (shell, cross-reference) |

### Port notes

- Handle geometry is "Pivot 5.2 verified" (comments in `canvas-interactions.mjs`): the two radius clamps (once in world units, again after the view scale), the colour table, the reverse paint/hit order, joined origins having no handle (the parent endpoint owns the joint) and the 8 px minimum hit radius are deliberate; do not simplify. Pointer hits use the nearest handle within radius; the square box test (`abs(dx) < r && abs(dy) < r`) is used when `nearest` is off.
- Handle descriptors are cached per figure by a settings key and invalidated on topology change (`invalidatePivotHandleTopology`); a dense scene (15,000 segments, 1,000 exposed handles) relies on lazy per-pose minima and the 32 px grid index above 512 handles. Keep a bounded index in native.
- The overlay redraws only damaged regions per figure group; during a figure move, moving figures are painted separately from the retained layer. Native should keep this split (cheap pose edits, no full-overlay redraw).
- Right-click is subtle: a press on a joint arms `maybe-bend`; 4 px of travel starts the bend and suppresses the menu; a stationary release opens the menu; some platforms send `contextmenu` on press, which is deferred. Shift+right bends immediately. A pen barrel button counts as right. Windows pen press-and-hold menus must be ignored.
- Dragging artwork never moves the figure (use the origin handle). Alt-click only selects; duplication begins only after 3 px.
- A modified (Shift/Ctrl) press on a handle must not author a pose until release or slop: it is a selection toggle first.
- Slop values: mouse 0, pen 5, touch 10; tweener 3; marquee minimum 3 px; Alt-drag duplicate 3 px; maybe-bend 4 px.
- Timings: wheel glide time constant 40 ms; wheel/pan idle 100 ms; GPU recovery 1 s then 5 s and "stable" after 30 s; fps HUD refreshes every 250 ms; chrome update at most every 200 ms; rail long press 450 ms (6 px); motion-path double-click window 450 ms; pen press-and-hold menu window 700 ms.
- Zoom clamps: 0.001..100 (wheel, pinch, menu steps); Fit clamps 0.01..2. Zoom keeps the Draw view's rotation and flip. A fitted view is tracked and refit on resize only while unmodified.
- Code versus docs: `tool-pose` (P) is a distinct command id whose behaviour equals Select (`['select','pose']` are always checked together), though docs only mention Select. `#viewport-hint` is hidden by `stage.css` but still written; the header mode message is the visible one. `docs/inverse-kinematics.md` notes that an old rig test still assumes R enters Rig, while the code assigns R to Handle Edit. `docs/hotkeys.md` says Space "adds a frame" in the simple timeline; in code Space plays or pauses and Add Frame is A. The Properties palette paging (32 rows) lives in the inspector area.
- `ik.mjs`, `spatial-mesh.mjs`, `spatial-mesh-legacy.mjs` and `spatial-projection.mjs` are now validation and read-view helpers only; solving, skinning and perspective are in the engine (`styk_eval::ik`, mesh draw list). The native port keeps only what the UI reads (chain length for the stepper, perspective availability, spatial pose reads).
- Spatial controls are markup views over engine commands (`setSpatialPose`, `setSpatialCamera`, `setSpatialProportions`, `setSpatialIKTarget`, `setSpatialMotion`, `setPoseChoice`, `setNodeIK`); the pad and each range input open one `beginDrag` gesture and finish on pointer up, lost capture or cancel; a selection change mid-gesture finishes it.
- Escape cancels in a fixed order: drawing, pick modes, keyboard handle gesture, held keys, timeline gesture, canvas drag, any leftover editor drag, join tool, menus.
- Cross-references (no rows here): Build workspace tools (line, chain, circle, polygon, rig, fills, outliner, construction snapping) and Build context menus; Draw view rotation/flip and its own pinch; camera outline and handle dragging (`camera-overlay.mjs`) and the Camera inspector; motion path trails, dragging and falloff (`motion-path*.mjs`, `docs/MOTION-PATHS.md`); onion skins; VFX gizmos; the timeline Edit selected frames button; inspector quick actions (To front, To back), the Arrange section and Properties panel layout; colour picker, palette and eyedropper loupe; project tabs; layout solver stage insets; command palette; tutorial strip hosting (`setLesson`); Add menu contents.
- The rotating cube recipe (`docs/ROTATING-CUBE.md`) is a Build authoring walk-through; the Animate-side behaviour is plain posing (drag Turn, Ctrl-drag to stretch Depth), and the Reference dialog belongs to Build.

## Camera, scene, motion paths, onion skins, text tool, sprites and polygons (CA)

Scope: the virtual camera (gizmo on the stage, numeric fields, output monitor, composition guides, neighbour-pose commands, baking into figures), camera motion clips (Follow, Shake, Handheld, Wobble, Impact punch, Zoom pulse) with their graph, timeline Motion row and bake, layer parallax, motion-path trails on the stage (display settings popover, ghost, drag, right-drag falloff reshape), onion skins (controller, settings popover, ruler range markers), the text tool dialog (T, Edit text), the Build sprite placement dialog and attachment mode, and the polygon construction tool (draft, preview, finish). Main files: `src/camera-overlay.mjs`, `src/camera-monitor.mjs`, `src/camera-motion*.mjs`, `src/layer-parallax.mjs`, `src/motion-path*.mjs`, `src/onion-*.mjs`, `src/text-dialog.mjs`, `src/text-authoring*.mjs`, `src/native-text-authoring.mjs`, `src/text-preview.mjs`, `src/text-font-compat.mjs`, `src/sprite-placement-dialog.mjs`, `src/polygon-draft.mjs`, `src/polygon-preview.mjs`; the stage wiring lives in `src/app.mjs` (pointer-down branches near `activeCameraHandle`, `pickMotionPath`, `polygonClick`, `beginSpritePlacement`, `showTextDialog`, `drawMotionPaths`, `onionSkins`). Contract docs: `docs/CAMERA-EDITING.md`, `docs/camera-baking.md`, `docs/camera-controls.md`, `docs/camera-motion.md`, `docs/MOTION-PATHS.md`, `docs/onion-skin.md`, `docs/text-authoring.md`, `docs/SPRITE-PLACEMENT.md`, `docs/polygon-construction.md`. Evaluation, sampling, planning and edits already live in the Rust engine (`styk_eval::camera`, `follow`, `parallax`; `styk-edit` `camera_commands.rs`, `camera_bake.rs`, `motion_path.rs`, `trails.rs`, `onion_skins.rs`); the native port owes the interaction, view models and drawing around them. Text outline layout (font parsing, glyph layout) is JavaScript today (opentype in a worker) and has to be re-homed.

### Port notes

- Code is the truth: `docs/camera-controls.md` says hit targets are 24 screen pixels; the code uses a 12 px radius (24 px across), consistent. `docs/onion-skin.md` and `docs/MOTION-PATHS.md` say Onion starts enabled and paths start hidden: both match the code (`state.onion=true`; `motionPaths` forced false when settings load). The motion path "Full" range is `motionPathRange = 0`.
- The Camera inspector has Transform and Motion tabs selected through `editor._cameraInspectorTab`. The DOM-id coupling (`data-motion-id`, `data-property`) is how scrubbing finds its owner today; replace it with typed view-model ids.
- The camera monitor, parallax preview and onion drawing already come from the engine (`drawCameraMonitor`, `posePicture`, `Stage::set_onions`). Native owns the widget and its redraw policy only: draw when visible, DPR cap 2, keep the last picture, 1.5 s back-off after a failure.
- Camera drag scale uses the ratio to the grab point, not absolute distance; rotation accumulates shortest-arc deltas so crossing the +/-180 degree seam does not jump. Snap rules: 10% scale, 15 degrees rotation.
- Camera outline dots are clipped to the viewport and keep their phase along the original perimeter (`offset % 6`). A native dashed stroker must be fed the same offset or dots crawl on pan.
- Timings: trail double-click 450 ms; trail pick radius 10 px (hover; 9 px default arg); camera monitor retry 1.5 s; text compile debounce 100 ms (0 on font change); onion scrub 8 px per frame (Shift 32); trail sampling budget 6 ms; falloff weight uses `u = d / (radius + 1)` so weights stay strictly positive inside the radius.
- Falloff drag: right button down on a trail suppresses the context menu for that gesture only; a stationary right-click elsewhere must still open the usual menu. Scroll during the drag must not zoom the stage. Pressing/releasing Shift mid-drag re-previews from the original pose.
- The Motion row hit-test has subtle rules: 7 px edge zones, the selected clip's edge reaches 3 px into lane gaps, the selected clip is painted last, and lanes are recomputed after every edit (`_refreshMotionRows` re-lays out rows only when lane membership or count changed).
- The motion graph keeps its scale monotonic during edits (max of previous and new) so reducing amplitude visibly shrinks the curve; "Fit curve" resets it. The cursor repaint is keyed on `tick:inside`. Follow clips resample whenever the document revision changes; other types only when clip data or fps change.
- Camera Motion Start is shown 1-based and stored 0-based. Slider range versus typed range differ intentionally (soft range) and the slider max grows when a larger value is typed.
- Text: font parsing and layout are JavaScript (opentype) in a worker today; native needs an equivalent (outline extraction, `sourceMetrics` estimated spacing for imported text, no kerning for imported text). Complex shaping, right-to-left and non-BMP characters are unsupported (rejected before Apply); keep the 16,384 / 255 / BMP limits and the Windows symbol-font mapping. The preview must draw vector outlines only.
- Text dialog guards: Escape while applying is ignored; closing terminates the worker immediately (rapid Escape/reopen must not keep the old session); pending requests reject as aborted; per-session font caps (8 faces, 32 MiB each, 64 MiB total) reject rather than evict.
- Sprite placement keeps signed 32-bit integer anchors and allows points outside the image; the preview fits both image and handles. Coincident handles are an error. PNG only, <= 16M pixels; drawing-cel figures are rejected.
- Polygon construction is transient (no history); shortest-path chaining follows the native algorithm (skips already selected route joints, does not search alternatives); closing is checked before Ctrl routing. Several app shortcuts are deliberately blocked while drafting (a safety policy not in the native manual).
- Cross-references (no rows here): the camera inspector layout and generic PropertyControls scrubbing belong to the inspector area; `timeline-rows.mjs` layout and clip context menus belong to the timeline area; Build-mode tools, Ctrl+D duplicate placement and the Build outliner belong to the Build area; export of camera-baked documents belongs to export; Scene row effects belong to VFX.

## Inspector, properties, styles, VFX and colour picker (IN)

Scope: the Properties panel (right dock) and everything it edits by value: the descriptor-driven inspector (`src/inspector-view.mjs` builds one descriptor per context, `src/inspector-kit.mjs` renders it, `src/inspector-header.mjs` draws the target line, scope line and quick-action host, `src/inspector-dom.mjs` reconciles the DOM, `src/inspector-bindings.mjs` applies a changed control through editor commands, `src/inspector-legacy.mjs` still serves Draw's tool markup), numeric scrubbing and gesture ownership (`src/property-controls.mjs`, `src/native-property-edit.mjs`), animated-style vocabulary and validation (`src/animation-*.mjs`, `src/editor-branch-style.mjs`), keyframe easing (`src/keyframe-easing*.mjs`), the Segment values and Background dialogs, the colour picker with its eyedropper, magnifier and palettes (`src/color-picker*.mjs`, `src/color-palette.mjs`), the whole VFX panel, clip timeline lanes, gizmos, catalogue, presets and key-channel filter (`src/vfx*.mjs`), the Tweener tool (`src/tweener-controls.mjs`) and the Build rig sections (`src/rig-controls.mjs`, `rig-control-details.mjs`, `rig-pose-choices.mjs`). Contracts: `docs/VFX.md`, `docs/VFX-DESIGN.md`, `docs/property-channels.md` (superseded by the layer sheet), `docs/keyframe-easing.md`, `docs/ANIMATED-STYLES.md`, `docs/TOPOLOGY-STYLES.md`, `docs/BRANCH-STYLE.md`, `docs/animation-origin.md`, `docs/segment-values.md`, `docs/engine/vfx.md`. In the native port the evaluation of effects, easing and animated styles is already Rust (`styk_eval::vfx`, `styk_eval::channels`); what remains to rebuild is the view-model layer (`styk-core::inspector`, `styk-core::vfx`), the widgets (`styk-ui::inspector`, `styk-design::widgets`) and the persisted preferences.

### Port notes

- Docs vs code: `docs/property-channels.md` is marked superseded (layer sheet writes full layer pose keys, no per-property key controls), but `Editor({propertyChannels:true})`, `animationChannels`, branch plans and the validators remain live compatibility code; the native port needs the read/validate/preserve path, not new per-property key UI. `docs/VFX-DESIGN.md` says the panel has "Properties, VFX, Library tabs" and "an empty target offers twelve starting points"; code shows 4 effects plus 2-3 presets plus 5 generators plus up to 3 My presets depending on the target. `docs/VFX-DESIGN.md` says "no separate VFX timeline", yet `vfx-clip-timeline.mjs` adds effect lanes when the panel is visible; follow the code. `docs/BRANCH-STYLE.md` names "Apply builder changes to branch" while the code label is "Apply to branch" (Animate) / Whole branch (Build).
- The inspector never shows key state (no diamonds); VFX parameters are the exception (a key button beside each effect value).
- Exact timings and limits: magnifier 30 samples/s, one in flight; user-preset saved flash 2200 ms; scrub dead zone 2 px (pen) and tween 3 px; tween 300 px per 100 % (3000 with Shift); easing popover 232 px wide; palette limits 64 project / 64 personal / 32 palettes / 12 recent / 60-char names; gradient stops 2-32; VFX stack 16, 64 simultaneous, 4096 clips, 512 key channels per clip, 1,000,000 keys; fills page 12, figure colours page 32; inspector budget per context: 30 controls, 5 open sections, 8 titled sections, 4 core sections (a dev audit, `inspectorBudgetProblems`; belongs in styk-devtools).
- Reconciliation by position is load-bearing in the web UI (hidden placeholders). A native retained-mode UI should key by identity (section id, row id) instead; keep the behaviour that a focused field is never overwritten and that open/closed state survives refreshes.
- Number fields are plain numeric inputs; the revision-4 expressions ("+10", "*2") were deferred and are NOT implemented. Do not add them silently, and do not drop the Mixed placeholder restore.
- A typed edit commits as one gesture; a rejected value must not enter the model (finite and `Math.fround`-finite checks mirror the f32 range). Percentages convert at the boundary: opacity 0-100 to 0-255 inverse bytes, segment/outline opacity channel 0-1, scale 100 = 1.
- Property edits are cancelled when the report/frame/selection changes mid-gesture; the VFX inspector commits instead of cancelling when history moves (`finishForHistory`). Preserve that difference.
- The colour picker opens from any `input[type=color]` or `[data-paint-picker]` by a document-level click capture; the resolver in `src/app.mjs` (`installColorPickers`) decides per control (draw colour, palette replacement, figure colour with opacity separated, segment paint, background, breadcrumb background). A native port needs one colour-edit request API with an anchor and a target token; Draw's colour rail and brush share the same palette store.
- The eyedropper consumes pointer events over the stage in capture so tools do not act; clicks outside the picker normally dismiss it, except while sampling. The sampler reads the engine's rendered frame (not DOM pixels), so it works with GPU rendering; the loupe is 11x11.
- Colour palettes: `rememberColor` runs only if the colour changed during the session; the "+" target depends on the view (Recent is never a target unless no view was ever chosen and the project has no colours, then the first save switches to Project).
- VFX evaluation, clip activity/tail (`vfxClips`), group membership (`vfxGroup`) and extents (`vfxExtent`) are already answered by the engine session (`editor._shown`); the native UI should ask the same questions instead of recomputing. Gizmo geometry is re-read by revision and tick.
- The key-channel filter and "selected effect" are view state, not document state; they reset on document change.
- Effects the legacy stack calls "pose effects" still exist for older projects; keep read/edit parity or migrate on open.
- Cross-references (no rows here): IK section view (`ik-view`), Spatial controls and pose-choice picker, camera motion pane and Parallax (`camera-motion-ui`), movieclip sections (`movieclip-ui`), builder surface (`builder-surface-ui`), drawing-view (Draw), the quick-actions controller (`ui/quick-actions`), the filmstrip that hosts the easing popover (timeline area), the Test rig UI (`rig-test-ui.mjs`, Build area), the `timeline-menu.mjs` menu widget and `ui-dialog` prompt/confirm.

## Timeline, layers, keyframes, playback, audio and movieclips (TL)

Scope: the Animate timeline in both of its views (the Advanced exposure sheet, `Timeline` in `src/layer-timeline.mjs`, and the Simple filmstrip, `SheetFilmstrip` in `src/sheet-filmstrip.mjs`), the shared timeline header and its popovers (`src/timeline-header.mjs`, mounted by `src/timeline-host.mjs`), the layer list (folders, search, focus, filter, drag to restack, colours, menus), the Simple Layers popover, playback range and transport readouts, audio lanes/import/mix/export (`src/audio-*.mjs`), movieclip creation, the movieclip inspector sections and the movieclip scope breadcrumb (`src/movieclip-ui.mjs`, `src/editor-movieclips.mjs`), and the read-side editor modules for layers, the layer sheet, focus and frame ids. Contracts: `docs/TIMELINE.md`, `docs/layer-timeline.md`, `docs/LAYER-FOLDERS.md`, `docs/LAYER-FOCUS.md`, `docs/AUDIO.md`, `docs/AUDIO-UX.md`, `docs/movieclips.md`, `docs/animation-modes.md`, `docs/hotkeys.md`. Several of these docs are partly stale (see Port notes): the code is the truth.

Cross-references (no rows here, owned elsewhere): camera motion rows/clips (`src/camera-motion-timeline.mjs`), effect (VFX) rows and clips (`src/vfx-clip-timeline.mjs`, `src/vfx-key-channels.mjs`), the keyframe easing popover and Bezier editor (`src/keyframe-easing-popover.mjs`, `src/keyframe-easing.mjs`), onion skin range markers on the ruler (`src/onion-timeline-range.mjs`, `src/onion-controller.mjs`), the touch timeline (`src/touch/timeline-scrubber.mjs`, `timeline-touch.mjs`), the Tweener tool (`docs/TWEENER.md`), the Draw workspace's drawing layers and cels, the shared clipboard (`src/shared-clipboard.mjs`), the thumbnail renderer (`THUMBNAIL-PERFORMANCE.md`), the dock/shell splitter that sizes the timeline panel, the command palette, Settings pages, export dialogs (the video "Include audio" option), and the stage play loop in `src/app.mjs` (its audio/packet coupling is listed in TL-081 to TL-086 only as far as the timeline owns it).

### Port notes

- The code is the truth where docs disagree. `docs/TIMELINE.md` still documents an "independent layer and property tracks" mode and a "whole-document frame editing" toolbar (Page Up/Down key visiting, Alt+Left/Right nudging, F6 insert, `setMode('tracks'|'frames')`, `selectTrack(...,{notify})` marquee select, Move dialog, Timing dialog, Bake dialog, Blank button). None of that exists in `layer-timeline.mjs` today (no marquee, no key nudge, no Move/Timing/Bake dialogs); the current Advanced grid is the exposure sheet only, and F6 now moves focus between regions (Shift+F6 clears keys). Do not port the removed surfaces. `docs/hotkeys.md` still says Ctrl+Enter is unassigned and A "duplicates the frame after the current frame": the real A behaviour is the key-vs-duplicate rule in TL-040.
- `docs/SIMPLE-FRAME-DRAFTS.md` and the Working frames section of `docs/animation-modes.md` describe features the interface no longer starts; only the stored-extension validation and Pivot export behaviour remain (TL-066). Native can skip the Add/Update/Discard UI but must keep reading and saving `_styk.simpleFrameDraft`.
- Row order trap: user layers are displayed front-most first, but the layer state order array is back to front (`order.toReversed()` in the drag code, `.reverse()` in `_reindex`). Camera and Scene are always above all user layers, pinned or scrolled with them depending on how much height they take.
- Frame numbering is 1-based in every label but ticks are 0-based; playback range is half-open `[startTick,endTick)` in state but inclusive 1-based in the popover; `selectedTimeRange.end` is exclusive.
- Selection range `start..end` is inclusive; hit-testing clamps ticks to 0..9,999,999 and allows selecting and seeking beyond the animation end (placing a key extends the duration; navigation alone does not).
- Move is only possible for a range selected before pointer-down; a press on an unselected cell always starts a new range. Moves and drags use a 4 px dead zone (Manhattan distance) for grid gestures and 4 px (Euclidean) for audio.
- Timing values to keep: edge scroll zone 28 px with 120 to 1200 px/s (layer list 10 px); label wheel glide time constant 70 ms and a 40 px threshold; Ctrl-wheel zoom `exp(-delta*0.0015)` clamped to ±240; audio fill timer 40 ms with 650 ms lookahead and 40 ms start offset; audio scrub snippet 80 ms throttled to 100 ms; thumbnail retry backoff 120 ms to 2 s; readout updated at 5 Hz while playing; hold-bar drag step is at least 2 px per inbetween.
- The header must not write to the DOM (native: must not relayout) while playing except for the readout; header fold decisions are cached and recomputed only on resize or label change. During filmstrip playback no card re-renders.
- Scrubbing uses `seek(evaluate:false)` and a single in-flight preparation; the host (not the timeline) decides when the exact frame is settled (`canScrub` waits for the render to be submitted). Keyboard seeks evaluate normally. Pointer release always flushes the final tick.
- Layer focus is not in the document: the editor sends the focused layer ids with each engine call and the engine refuses edits elsewhere with the same message as a saved lock; copies used for save, export and playback never inherit it. Lock buttons are disabled for focus-locked layers; `savedLocked` and `focusLocked` are separate fields.
- View-only state (find query, focus, filter, collapsed folders, Edit-together flag, playback range, audio panel open, audio selection, Auto-key button state mirrors a saved preference) resets on document load; only `timelineMode`, `autoKey` and `replaceKeyframesOnDrop` persist (settings). The label column width, zoom and row heights are not persisted (the bottom panel height is part of the dock layout).
- Audio is in seconds, not frames; clip edits can extend past the animation end, while playback and export obey the animation range. Rendering shares the same mixer for preview and export (full-scale saturation, not limiter). AAC priming samples are not trimmed in browser MP4 export; a native encoder should decide this explicitly. Native app should drop the 8 MiB/2 min browser fallback and the Blob cache design, but keep the 32 MiB/96 MiB/1 h/64/4096/32 limits unless deliberately changed.
- Auto key is forced off in Simple mode even when the preference is on; the policy lives in the editor (`editor.autoKey`) and applies at every place that chooses a held cell's key, so a native port needs it in `styk-edit`, not only in the timeline UI.
- Movieclip clocks are by presence, not by placement: do not reuse scene-frame arithmetic for clip time. Movieclips are never placed on drawing layers; a drawing-only clip creates a new figure layer named after the clip.
- Layer sheet edits are atomic engine commands; the interface may batch several (`movieclipBatch`) into one undo step with a label (examples: "Change easing", "Remove scene frames", "Set frame inbetweens", "Extend keyframe", "Import audio and extend animation", "Show all layers", "Unlock all layers"). Undo step names should be preserved.
- Memory and cache bounds that affect feel: lazy tables cache 64 keyframes and 1024 figure types; filmstrip bitmap cache 32 canvases (320x192) with eviction that frees pixels; engine playback tickets 120; label row nodes recycled; audio waveforms at most 4096 bins and only the visible pixels are drawn; the Advanced grid draws only visible rows and columns for 20,000+ layers and 1.2 billion ticks.
- Cross-area items to coordinate: camera motion and VFX row painters share `timeline-rows.mjs` geometry and the same canvas; onion ranges draw on the ruler; the easing popover (Bezier handles) is used by both timelines; the touch timeline replaces gestures and row sizes; thumbnails come from the shared thumbnail renderer; the playback loop and play button state live in `app.mjs` (stage/frames area) but depend on the timeline's range and the audio gate.

## Draw workspace (DR)

The Draw workspace is a preset of the one editor: it reuses the tool rail, Properties inspector, stage header, timeline, clipboard and history, and adds a raster drawing layer type. Cels are sparse 64x64 tile stores (never dense images) published into the document as `styk_tiles` blobs; the Rust drawing engine (`styk-drawing`) does every brush, fill, filter, selection and codec job. The main JS files are `src/drawing-controller.mjs` (all gestures, selection, operations, quick bar, pop-up palette), `src/drawing-view.mjs` (inspector, quick bar, palette markup, presets), `src/drawing-brush.mjs` / `drawing-sliders.mjs` / `drawing-pressure-curve.mjs` (settings), `src/drawing-resources.mjs` + `drawing-tiles.mjs` + `drawing-history*.mjs` + `drawing-preparation.mjs` (stores, memory, undo, publication), `src/engine/engine-drawing*.mjs`, `engine-owner-drawing.mjs`, `engine-live-cels.mjs` (the bridge to the engine and its stage), `src/editor-drawing.mjs` (target cel), `src/pen-input.mjs` and the Draw branches of `src/app.mjs` and `src/studio-shell.mjs`. Contracts: `docs/DRAWING.md`, `docs/DRAWING-PLAN.md`, `docs/engine/drawing.md`, `docs/hotkeys.md`. Engine-side work (brush model, raster, fills, filters, codecs, live cels in `styk-eval::live`) already lives in Rust: those rows say "engine as is" and the native work is the front end, input and platform glue. Timeline rows for drawing layers (A/Shift+A/F7, Next copy, DRAW badge, cel thumbnails, onion modes) belong to the timeline area; they are cross-referenced in Port notes only.

### Screens and states to capture

| Screen ID | States | How to reach it |
|---|---|---|
| draw.stage | Default (checkerboard artboard with cel), brush cursor ring, wet ink in progress, canvas with floating selection and handles, mirrored axes, view rotated/flipped, loading cel (pointer waiting), operation running | Open a document with a drawing layer; switch workspace to Draw; select Brush and drag |
| draw.rail | Default; each tool selected; hover; pressed; corner tick hover; disabled (no document) | Draw workspace left rail; right-click or long press Select pixels / Shapes for the variants |
| menu.draw-rail-variants | Select pixels variants (Lasso, Rectangle, Ellipse, Wand), Shapes variants (Line, Rectangle, Ellipse), current radio checked | Right-click or long-press the rail tool, or click its corner tick |
| menu.add | Draw version with "New drawing layer" first row | Draw workspace, rail + corner tick |
| draw.quickbar | Brush, Eraser, Fill (with colour rail), Select pixels (shape choices), Shape (kind choices), Transform/Pan (flip/reset); presets grid open; narrow fit (tight, tighter, scrolling); slider hover/drag/editing; hidden when not in Draw | Stage header in Draw at 1280 px and at about 640 px stage width |
| draw.quickbar.colors | Colour pair, rail with 8 swatches, "+N", "+" save, selected swatch, project/recent/personal palette labels, no colours | Stage header, Fill or Brush tool; add project colours first for +N |
| draw.slider | Default, hover, focus, dragging, inline typing, disabled-by-clamp, log vs linear | Any slider in quick bar or Properties; double-click to type |

| draw.inspector.empty | Layer hidden or locked, no exposure, not a drawing layer, first-use hint | Draw on a hidden/locked drawing layer; or a blank exposure |

### Port notes

- The code is the truth where docs differ: the plan lists an Eyedropper tool, but the code has none (Alt-click picks); `docs/DRAWING.md` calls Shift+M "horizontal" while `mirrorX` mirrors across the vertical centre line and the inspector's "Mirror vertically" is `mirrorY`; the quick bar is created inside the viewport and then re-parented into the stage header (`stageHead.adopt`), so comments about "until it moves" are stale.
- Draw shortcuts live in scope `drawing`, active only when focus is outside `.layer-timeline`/`.sheet-filmstrip`; Delete is deliberately unbound in the drawing scope and rerouted to `delete-pixels` only when a pixel selection exists. Copy/cut/paste similarly yield to the timeline's frame clipboard. Builder also uses Shift+Backspace (scope builder), so scopes must be kept separate.
- Timings: HUD 900 ms; wet ink redraw floor 8 ms; palm window 700 ms; pen/touch slop 5/10 px; pen press-and-hold menu guard 700 ms; live-cel feed 6 ms and 96 tiles per frame; picture upload budget 512 KiB / 250 ms, 2 MiB / 1.5 s, then 8 MiB; history disk request timeout 30 s; stroke batches 512 dabs (8,192 for tiny tips); quick bar rebuilds per animation frame only when markup changes.
- Exactness is a contract: Rust brush output is byte-frozen against recorded fixtures; use `styk-math` for sin/cos/atan2/hypot/pow; arithmetic stored in documents must not use `f64::sin`. The native port should call `styk-drawing` directly (no WASM or workers needed) but must keep cancel semantics (drop the job, discard late results) and the bounded memory budgets.
- Undo is the engine session's entry naming a pixel record; the front end must apply tile buffers and the session undo in one synchronous step, and cold records (disk tier) need an async replay that can be cancelled without changing pixels or the cursor. The IndexedDB tier is temporary and must be replaced by a session-scoped temp directory that is purged at startup; saved documents never depend on it.
- Publication is derived work, not an edit: never mark the document dirty because tiles were encoded; save/close/recovery/export must await it (`drawingBarrier`).
- Pointer pitfalls: stroke samples must be de-duplicated by timestamp across raw and regular move events; a gesture interrupted by capture loss keeps its ink; a pointer pressed during an operation is queued (`loading.queued`) and replayed; two-finger navigation cancels a provisional finger stroke; pen tilt becomes a screen lean vector (16 px) mapped through view and cel pose.
- The selection outline (marquee, polygon or mask) is canonical; the floating selection derives from it. A click without drag in Select pixels deselects; tool change away from lasso settles the float; a moved selection auto-applies on frame navigation, timeline click, mode change and save.
- Smudge is special: no taper-out, no texture, always the round tip; blend `smudge` is ignored by the inspector preview (previews as normal).
- Presets are whole-tip definitions; changing any tip number sets `preset=null` (except alpha lock); Brush and Eraser keep separate tip memory but share the pressure curve and colours. Brush library size limit 48; recent colours 12 (project 64, personal 64, 32 personal palettes) are owned by the palette area.
- Cross-references (no rows here): drawing rows, DRAW badge, cel thumbnails, Blank/Next copy/Copy here menus, onion skin with drawing keys and the layer filmstrip are the timeline area; the colour picker popover internals and palettes are the colour area; settings dialog layout (`settings.interface`) is the settings area; stage zoom/pan/rotation mechanics and `canvas-pinch` are the stage area; movieclip drawing banks' runtime selection is the movieclip area.
- The engine renders the stage in the app; a native iced front end must draw live cels through `styk-render` rather than a UI canvas, and keep the overlay (selection, handles, cursor, axes, shapes) as a UI layer in screen space. Wet ink is optional if the native pen latency is already below one frame, but the retire-after-presented-stamp rule must hold if it is kept.

## Build workspace: figure construction, Structure panel, rig setup and rig test (BU)

Build (internally `mode = 'rig'`, `state.builderActive`, the band's "Build" session) is a bounded editing session on one figure definition. It is entered by choosing a construction tool (Edit joints, Line, Chain pen, Circle, Polygon, Attach) or "Edit in Build"; it is left with Done, which asks what to do with the work. Inside it the person draws lines, circles, chains and fills, rearranges the part tree in the Structure panel, splits, duplicates, flips and orders parts, sets up rigs (connectors, Smart Stretch, response, angle locks, weighted joints, angle-driven paths, 3D spatial articulation) and tests the rig in a detached preview dialog. Main files: `src/builder-mode.mjs` (controller, exit flow, context menu host), `src/builder-session.mjs` (bounded session and exit transactions), `src/builder-outliner.mjs` (Structure), `src/builder-construction.mjs` (pen drafts and smart snapping), `src/builder-aids.mjs`, `src/builder-appearance.mjs`, `src/builder-fill.mjs`, `src/builder-duplicate-preview.mjs`, `src/builder-context-menu.mjs`, `src/builder-surface-ui.mjs`, `src/editor-builder-style.mjs`, `src/rig-test-ui.mjs`, `src/rig-examples*.mjs`, `src/rig-mechanism-examples.mjs`, `src/rig-solids.mjs`, the rig validators (`rig-constraints`, `rig-spatial`, `rig-weighted`, `rig-angle-path`, `movieclip-mesh`), `src/rig-controls.mjs` (the Rig sections of Properties) and the Build parts of `src/app.mjs` (pointer handling, tool hints, action map). Contracts: `docs/BUILDER-AIDS.md`, `docs/BUILDER-ORDER.md`, `docs/BUILDER-ROADMAP.md` (proposals only, none implemented beyond what is listed), `docs/RIG-CONSTRAINTS.md`, `docs/engine/rigs-ik.md`, `docs/hotkeys.md`. The edit commands themselves (`splitBuilderSegments`, `moveBuilderSegment`, `moveBuilderOrder`, `addSegments`, `addConnector`, `setRigBehavior`, `enableSmartStretch`, `addWeightedJoint`, ...) live in the Rust `styk-edit` session (`engine/crates/styk-edit/src/builder.rs`) reached through `src/engine/engine-commands.mjs`; the native `styk-core::build` must reuse them, not reimplement them. Rig evaluation (`styk_eval::rig`, `ik`) is engine-owned and is not ported here.

Native owner shorthand used below: `styk-core::build` (session, tools, snapping, construction drafts), `styk-ui::build` (Build surfaces), `styk-ui::panels::structure`, `styk-ui::dialogs::rig_test`, `styk-ui::dialogs::rig_examples`, `styk-ui::dialogs::build_exit`. Engine owners are named when the engine already owns the behaviour.

### Port notes

- The code is the truth where docs disagree: `docs/BUILDER-AIDS.md` says the Builder background "initially inherits the scene"; the code keeps `builderCanvasColor` default `#ffffff` with `useBuilderCanvasColor` as the on/off switch, and writing the colour turns the switch on (`settings.mjs` L708). `docs/BUILDER-ROADMAP.md` lists proposals (tangent snapping, symmetry, branch library, freehand fitting) that are not implemented; do not inventory them as features. `docs/hotkeys.md` lists Shift+H/V flips and Ctrl+D as Build shortcuts, but `hotkeys.mjs` registers them as the generic `mirror`, `mirror-vertical` commands; `duplicate-segment` has no default key row in the `builder` group.
- A session is not a document snapshot: discard works by undoing every entry with `beforeRevision >= baselineRevision`, and the fallback repair exists only for history eviction. The native build must keep revisions on commands or provide an equivalent bookmark; "changed" is derived from the undo stack.
- Binding is by figure definition (`typeId`), not by instance: every instance on every frame is "bound", and a missing instance on the current frame is auto-created. Selection of anything else is rewritten by `enforceSelection` on every editor change.
- Selection confinement and auto-insertion are paused during the exit transaction (`resolving`); a porter who reacts to change events without this flag will recreate the figure while it is being discarded.
- Snapping constants: screen radius 8 px (joint/axis/length), 10 px for the stroke start joint, latched hysteresis 1.4x, chain duplicate-point filter 1 screen px, rig-test pick 14 px, outliner drag threshold 5 px, right-press bend threshold 4 px, drop zones 30/40/30 percent of the 30 px row. Alt bypasses snapping and clears the latch.
- Equal-length snap only applies when the stroke is longer than the snap radius, and only if no joint/midpoint/axis snap won; the order matters for feel.
- The chain pen draft path and the Path2D marker sets must only be rebuilt on click, Backspace or zoom change; rebuilding per pointer move was removed for speed. Keep the draft in world units and project at paint time.
- Chain Enter/Escape/Backspace are captured in the capture phase with `stopImmediatePropagation`, so they must pre-empt global shortcuts (Escape would otherwise cancel playback or selection) but not fire inside inputs, dialogs or open popovers.
- Stroke geometry divides by `abs(figure.scale)` and adds pi for negative scale; zero scale is a user-visible error. Circle drags share the same line data (length/angle) with a circle type.
- Whole-branch scope has three sources (toggle, Shift held, a branch already selected) and each context menu and inspector strip reads them separately; the toggle is reset when a branch-affecting selection changes (`state.builderBranch = false` at L3119 and L703).
- Draw order uses ONE sequence for segments and polygons; order values in the inspector are 1-based positions, but stored `z_order` can be tied or sparse. Animated figures (layer-sheet timelines) resolve the instance figure before reading order and begin edits per drawable target.
- The Structure signature skips rebuilding unless figure id, revision, or counts change; selection highlight re-renders every call. Native widgets should do the same incremental update; 30000-part figures are a supported case (virtualised 30 px rows).
- Context-menu rows are tuples `[command, icon, label, shortcut, disabled]`; labels carry message keys for relocalisation. Shortcut glyph text in the Build menu is hard-coded text ("Shift Del", "Ctrl Del", "F9"), not derived from the registry; derive it from bindings natively.
- The rig test intentionally runs on a separate engine session and editor; nothing it does enters the project's history. It must keep that isolation, and the "Add to Animate" path is the only way its pose becomes document data.
- Gallery thumbnails are painted strictly one per frame after becoming visible, so opening the dialog does not stall; keep a bounded queue and cancel on close.
- Rig validation (`rig-constraints`, `rig-spatial`, `rig-weighted`, `rig-angle-path`, `movieclip-mesh`) duplicates what the Rust engine validates at open (`styk_eval::rig::validate_2d`); native should call the engine validator and drop the JavaScript copies, keeping the user-facing error strings (`errors.rigConstraints.*`).
- Rig sections are always produced as three descriptors in fixed order (Rig, Attachment, Reference positions) with `hidden` flags so the inspector's positions stay put; keep the stable-position behaviour.
- The Rig section's controls are identified by DOM ids (`rig-*`) and routed through one change handler; the native inspector view model should keep the same ids as command parameters so tests port directly.
- The spatial gizmo and motion paths are off in Build; handles show static endpoints per `builderShowsStaticHandles` (always for drawing tools).
- Cross-references (no rows here): the sprite placement dialog and image decoding (import area), the segment values dialog (inspector area), IK controls and solvers (animation/engine area), generic inspector widgets (inspector area), layout/docking of the Structure panel (shell area), the exact-values dialog, library insert and paste (library/clipboard areas), Draw workspace exclusions (drawing area).
- Behavioural facts to confirm against the Rust command set: `moveBuilderSegment`, `splitBuilderSegments`, `moveBuilderOrder`, `setBuilderOrder`, `setPolygonOpacity`, `updateBuilderSegmentStyle`, `addSegments`, `addConnector`, `addWeightedJoint`, `bindStretchParts`, `setRigBehavior`, `enableSmartStretch`, `setAngleLock`, `setSecondaryAngleReference`, `setConnectorPosition`, `discardNewFigure`, `replaceFigureDefinition`, `importFigure` are called by the interface here; each must exist in the native command registry with undo and redo.

## Home, project library, libraries, samples and tutorials (HO)

Scope: the Home page (the app's start page over the editor), the desktop project list behind it, recent files, the figure and movieclip libraries (My library on disk or in browser storage, the Community library from stykanimate.com, the in-project figure list `AssetLibrary`), library drag and drop, hover previews, samples and the five tutorials. Main files: `src/home/{home,home-model,home-view}.mjs`, `src/tutorials/*`, `src/user-library.mjs` (the Library panel), `src/asset-library.mjs`, `src/web-library.mjs`, `src/community-library.mjs`, `src/library-{drag,files,hover-preview,matte}.mjs`, `src/demo.mjs`, `src/startup-document.mjs`, `src/startup-blue-data.mjs`, `desktop/{project-library,recent-files,public-library,library-store,samples}.cjs`. Contracts: `docs/HOME.md`, `docs/TUTORIALS.md`, `docs/asset-library.md`. Panel docking, the Insert dialog frame, the Settings dialog frame, the command wrapper, toasts, menus, prompts, import/export and canvas rendering belong to other areas and are only cross-referenced in Port notes.

| Save keys, command palette, Settings, Help (`?`), release notes | Allowed on Home | Home | `HOME_COMMANDS` |

| Mod+Tab (Ctrl+Tab on macOS) | Last used project | Anywhere | `hotkeys.mjs` (`recent-project`) |
| All other editor keys | Blocked while Home shows | Home | `home.mjs` (`blocksKey`) |

| Right-click | Project context menu on a card; folder menu on a folder chip | Home | `home-view.mjs` (contextmenu) |
| Arrow keys, Home, End | Move selection across library cards (row-wise geometry) | Library grid | `user-library.mjs` (`onKey`, `neighbour`) |
| Enter | Open folder or insert asset (`library-open`) | Library grid | `hotkeys.mjs` (group Library); `user-library.mjs` |

| Escape | Clear figure search | Insert dialog figure search | `asset-library.mjs` |

| Enter / Space | Add the focused figure | Insert dialog list | `asset-library.mjs` (`onKey`) |
| Click, drag, double-click on cards | Select; drag to move or insert; open or insert | Library | `user-library.mjs`; `library-drag.mjs` |

| Space (held) and drag; wheel or Ctrl+wheel | Pan and zoom, demonstrated by Show me | Tutorial "Move around the canvas" | `tutorial-catalog.mjs` (demos) |

### Port notes

- Code versus docs: `docs/asset-library.md` describes the Library panel and the "This project" source as one spec; in code the Library panel (`UserLibrary`) has only My library and Community, while "This project" figures are a separate component (`AssetLibrary`) in the Insert dialog plus Add menu rows. Home's Escape also clears a non-empty search first; the docs do not say so.
- Two different recent lists exist: `recent-files.json` (max 20, all file kinds including figures and clips, feeds Open recent and the OS recent documents) and Home's `projects.json` (max 200, animations only, with pins, folders and pictures). Both are fed from `rememberRecent`. Keep both; do not merge.
- Timings to preserve: picture capture 1800 ms after save or open; library status fade 6000 ms; hover preview delay 180 ms, frame cap 24 fps, stall timeouts 60 s then 15 s; tutorial advance 1300 ms, first nudge 7000 ms then every 11000 ms, mark follow 700 ms (1200 ms at step start), settle readings 320 and 1200 ms; community catalogue recheck 10 min; library selection sync debounce 250 ms while seeking; Show me glide 620 ms.
- Limits: Home pictures cached 96, library thumbnails 160, project figure previews 64 (8 to 128), posters 16 MiB, files 32 MiB, download ledger 5000, projects 200, folders 48, folder name 60, library item name 120, recent files 20.
- Opening a listed project that is already a document must reactivate its tab, not re-import. Home's picture comes from the project tab's existing bitmap and is accepted only for the document's own file; the native app should store the tab picture and not render a new one on save.
- Tutorials depend on controls of other areas (stage head, timeline toolbar, tool rail, dock tabs, zoom label, onion and play buttons, inspector Flip button, brand, workspace switch, undo button). The native port needs stable target IDs for them (a registry replacing CSS selectors), and `styk-core::tutorials` should read the probe values from core state rather than from the UI.
- Tutorial dirty suppression writes `editor.dirty` directly. Native should offer an explicit ephemeral-document flag (no recovery copy, no close prompt) and restore the person's timeline mode afterward.
- Folder labels are case-insensitively unique; deleting a folder never touches files; a project's folder is validated against existing folders on update.
- "Last opened" order uses `openedAt`, which updates on every open and save; "Last changed" uses file mtime, so they can differ.
- Community posters are WebP and My library keeps PNG sidecars: the native image service needs a WebP decoder. The download ledger counts the first download per asset only; a failed request must not be recorded.
- Samples exist only when running from source (packaged apps list none); whether the native app ships example animations is a product decision recorded here so it is not dropped silently.
- `AssetLibrary` complexity limits protect the interface thread; keep equivalent limits when drawing figure thumbnails natively.
- Cross-references (other areas): the Settings dialog frame and full settings list, the command palette, the stage header slot and `solveMessage`, menus, popovers and toast widgets, import (`readImportFile`, `acceptNativeFile`), window title, dirty state and close prompts, Build and Draw workspace switching, the Insert dialog frame and the Rig examples dialog, project tabs (`projectTabsView.picture`), update, feedback and What's new flows.

## Export, import and clipboard (EX)

Scope: everything that turns the open document into a file (video, animated image, still frames, frame sequences, editable Pivot or StykAnimate files), everything that brings outside files in (Pivot `.piv`/`.stk`, StykAnimate `.styk`/`.stkz`/`.stykmov`, Stick Nodes `.nodes`/`.nodemc`/`.stknds`/`.zip`, sprite images, backgrounds, audio clips), the shared (cross-window) clipboard and the preview/decoded-image machinery that exports and imports lean on. Main files: `src/export-sheet.mjs` (the dialog), `src/export-request.mjs` (request model, validation, names), `src/export-flow.mjs` (commands, progress, saving), `src/export-presets.mjs`, `src/export-capabilities.mjs`, `src/export-progress.mjs`, `src/export-sink.mjs`, `src/video-export.mjs`, `src/native-video-export.mjs`, `src/vfw-*.mjs`, `src/image-export.mjs`, `src/image-codecs.mjs`, `src/engine/engine-export*.mjs`, `src/document-export*.mjs`, `src/import-*.mjs`, `src/sticknodes/**`, `src/media-authoring.mjs`, `src/shared-clipboard.mjs`, `src/clipboard-wire.mjs`, `src/engine/engine-clipboards.mjs`, `src/native-files.mjs`, `src/native-file-codec.mjs`, `src/codec-*.mjs`. Contracts: `docs/EXPORT-FORMATS.md`, `docs/export-experience.md`, `docs/export-sampling.md`, `docs/gif-export.md`, `docs/image-exports.md`, `docs/document-exports.md`, `docs/WINDOWS-CODECS.md`, `docs/IMPORT-PIPELINE.md`, `docs/EDITOR-IMPORT.md`, `docs/SPRITE-IMPORT.md`, `docs/sticknodes-import.md`, `docs/CLIPBOARD-WIRE.md`, `docs/native-file-format.md`. In the native app the Rust engine already owns frame drawing (`styk-render`, `styk-raster`), SVG output (`styk-export`), container and Pivot codecs (`styk-formats`, `styk-piv`) and clipboard sessions (`styk-edit`); this inventory is mostly the interface, orchestration and platform half. Test names below are the files named by the docs or found in the code; verify the exact filename before relying on one.
