### Screens and states to capture

| Screen ID | States | How to reach it |
|---|---|---|
| dialog.export | closed, open (default, Video category), light and dark, 960 px wide, window 1100x680, compact 760x640, minimal 420x700; scrolled with edge fade | Open an animation with at least 8 frames; File > Export or Mod+Shift+E |
| dialog.export.preview | stage default (first frame), loading, soft (pictures loading), playing (pause icon), vertical letterbox (9:16), single-frame type (current frame), no frames | Export dialog; TikTok preset on a 16:9 canvas for letterbox; press play |
| dialog.export.trim | default full range, handles dragged, handle focused, invalid range (error and red fields), Simple-mode played-frames note, working-pose note, one-frame animation (strip hidden) | Export dialog; drag handles or type From/To |
| dialog.export.presets | none chosen, each of 8 chosen, keyboard focus, fit note, bars warning note, preset left after an edit | Export dialog top; click a tile; change Size to leave a preset |
| dialog.export.form | each category (Video, GIF, Frames, Project); each Type with its hint; Size Original/preset/Custom with lock; Quality Draft/Great/Max; JPEG quality; Dither on/off; Sound on/off/unavailable; Picture smoothing options and Transparent reason; disabled types with reason; invalid name, size, memory plan | Export dialog; change Format and Type; use a document with audio and a sprite for Sound and Dither |
| dialog.export.encoding | collapsed (summary in title), expanded, MP4 codec, AVI codec, manual bitrate with error, lossless (no quality), padded note | Export dialog > Video > MP4 / AVI > Encoding |
| dialog.export.vfw | codec list loading, ready, not ready, no compatible codec, Configure open (vendor window), custom settings label, quality/keyframes/rate disabled | Windows desktop build; Video > Windows AVI |
| dialog.export.foot | summary video, summary single frame, summary blank (Project), primary enabled, primary disabled with notice, inline error | Export dialog bottom |
| dialog.export.progress | preparing (indeterminate), rendering, GIF pass 1 of 2 and pass 2, finishing, saving, cancelling | Click Export on a 100+ frame animation; GIF for passes |
| dialog.export.stop | Keep exporting focused, Stop exporting hover, withdrawn when the export ends | Escape during an export |
| toast.exported | "Exported NAME" toast; project variant | Complete any export |
| toast.imported | "Imported NAME" for figure files, sprites, backgrounds | Import a .stkz or an image |
| menu.file.import | Import submenu: Import figures, Import sprite image, Import background | File > Import |
| overlay.drop-target | file drag hover over the window | Drag a file from the OS onto the window |
| dialog.sticknodes-entry | list of 3+ entries, entry hover/focus, cancel | Open a Stick Nodes `.zip` |
| dialog.import-notes | Pivot variant, Stick Nodes variant, long list scroll | Open a `.stknds` with effects, or a Pivot file with repaired identities |
| dialog.sprite-placement | default (Origin selected), End selected, dragging, error (non-integer), disabled apply, narrow (max 440 px) | Figure builder > add sprite image |
| overlay.sprite-placement-hint | crosshair cursor and viewport hint "Choose an attachment joint" | After accepting the sprite placement dialog |
| settings.playback | Playback quality select with 5 options | Settings > Playback |

### Shortcuts

| Keys | Action | Context | Where (code) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mod+Shift+E | Open the Export dialog | Editor | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`export`) |
| none by default | Import figures (rebindable) | Editor | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`import`) |
| Mod+C / Mod+X / Mod+V | Copy / cut / paste selection or frames (to the OS clipboard, cross-window) | Editor; timeline focus routes to frames | `src/hotkeys.mjs`; `src/app.mjs` |
| Left / Down, Right / Up | Move the trim handle 1 frame | Export trim handle focused | `src/export-sheet.mjs` |
| Shift + arrows | Move the trim handle 10 frames | same | `src/export-sheet.mjs` |
| PageUp / PageDown | Move the trim handle 10 frames | same | `src/export-sheet.mjs` |
| Home / End | Start handle to frame 1 or end handle to last frame; the other direction clamps to the opposite handle | same | `src/export-sheet.mjs` |
| Arrow keys | Move the focused sprite handle 1 image pixel | Sprite placement canvas focused | `src/sprite-placement-dialog.mjs` |
| Escape | Close the dialog, or ask "Stop exporting?" while exporting; cancel sprite attachment | Export dialog; viewport during sprite placement | `src/export-sheet.mjs`; `src/app.mjs` |
| Arrow keys | Roving focus between preset tiles | Export presets | `src/ui/roving-focus.mjs` |
| Enter | Activate the primary Export | Export dialog form | `src/ui/ui-dialog.mjs` |
| Primary-button drag | Move trim handle / sprite handle | Export strip; sprite preview | `src/export-sheet.mjs`; `src/sprite-placement-dialog.mjs` |

### Platform features used

| Feature | Where | Native owner |
|---|---|---|
| WebCodecs `VideoEncoder` / `VideoFrame` (VP8, VP9, H.264 `isConfigSupported`) | `src/export-capabilities.mjs`; `src/video-export.mjs` | styk-platform::video |
| WebCodecs `AudioEncoder` (Opus, AAC) | `src/audio-encode.mjs` | styk-platform::audio |
| FFmpeg subprocess service through Electron IPC (capabilities, framed PNG chunks, save) | `src/native-video-export.mjs`; `desktop/` | styk-platform::video |
| Windows Video for Windows helper subprocess and the vendor Configure dialog (owner HWND) | `src/vfw-*.mjs`; `native/vfw-bridge.cpp` | styk-platform::video |
| Web Workers, OffscreenCanvas, `createImageBitmap`, ImageBitmap transfer | `src/image-export.mjs`; `src/media-image-worker.mjs`; `src/preview-image-worker.mjs`; `src/engine/engine-export-worker.mjs` | styk-platform::image; styk-engine |

| Desktop shared clipboard IPC (`beginSharedClipboardWrite/Read`, chunked) and clipboard text IPC | `src/shared-clipboard.mjs`; `src/app.mjs` | styk-platform::clipboard |

| AudioContext / OfflineAudioContext `decodeAudioData`, HTMLMediaElement metadata | `src/audio-engine.mjs` | styk-platform::audio |
| `<dialog>` kit, `<progress>`, ARIA radiogroup / slider, `aria-live` status | `src/export-sheet.mjs`; `src/export-progress.mjs` | styk-ui::dialogs; styk-design::widgets |
| `navigator.deviceMemory` hint and user-agent sniff for the Safari pixel limit | `src/export-memory.mjs`; `src/export-sampling.mjs` | not needed natively (use real RAM) |
| zstd / zlib through WebAssembly (`styk-formats`) | `src/native-file-codec.mjs` | styk-formats |
| `requestAnimationFrame` and timers (250 ms progress repaint, 150 ms probe debounce) | `src/export-sheet.mjs`; `src/export-progress.mjs` | styk-ui |
| `Intl.NumberFormat` for counts, sizes, durations | `src/export-request.mjs`; `src/export-progress.mjs` | styk-core::i18n |
| Editor setting `exportPreferences` persisted with settings | `src/export-flow.mjs` | styk-platform::storage |

### Port notes

- Code is truth over docs: `docs/export-experience.md` still describes Windows AVI as a tile; in code it is a Type entry (`vfw`) in Video, listed only when `getVfwCapabilities` exists. The old export-panel field-id names survive only as test hooks (`EXPORT_FIELD_IDS`).
- The dialog holds one immutable `ExportRequest` replaced on every edit; visibility, validation, summary and primary label are pure functions of it. Port that shape as a pure `styk-core::export` model with the UI only rendering it. `tests/export-request.test.mjs` compares against about 47,000 legacy combinations; keep an equivalent test.
- Quirks to keep: a hidden JPEG quality never leaks into other formats (constant 0.94); manual bitrate still passes the preset quality; dither and smoothing are passed for every format, SVG forces `edges`; GIF repeat follows the playback Loop setting unless set; From and To count played frames even in Simple mode.
- Deliberate differences already shipped: a remembered custom size of another shape opens as Original; a command run after the dialog closed exports defaults, not leftovers; the figure entry writes `.stykmov` for a selected movieclip.
- Timings: probe debounce 150 ms; progress repaint 250 ms; soft-preview retry 250 ms x 40; export worker warm 60 s; document export worker reuse 30 s; codec worker idle stop 2 s; browser blob URL revoked after 10 s; import timeout 180 s; sprite decode timeout 120 s; Automatic playback quality 2 s down, 6 s up, 2.5 s hold.
- Output limits are real behaviour (65,534 ZIP frames, 16,384 px side, 64 Mpx, 16 GiB streamed, 4 GiB ZIP32, GIF at most 50 fps). The native app need not keep the 512 MiB in-memory bound but should keep messages for the limits it keeps.
- A cancelled or failed export must never leave a truncated destination: stage beside the destination and rename; native video moves disk-to-disk.
- The native app can drop: SharedArrayBuffer cancel flags, MessagePort codec transport and codec worker host, browser MP4 muxer fallback and Safari pixel limit, the Python oracle, the download-anchor save, `deviceMemory`. It must keep: encoders for H.264, VP9 with alpha, MPEG-4, FFV1, raw, WMV8 (FFmpeg-equivalent), VFW on Windows, exact odd-size padding, black matte rules, and sound muxing.
- The shared clipboard must stay cross-window; kinds are figures, sheet, drawing-pixels and camera-motion; the audio clip clipboard is lazy and session-local.
- Cross-references only (no rows here): Save, Save As, recovery and tab handling belong to documents; the figure builder's entry for adding a sprite, the audio timeline clip UI, and the Settings screens belong to their own areas; the generic dialog kit (`ui-dialog`) belongs to styk-design.

## Settings, shortcuts, support, privacy, updates, localization, touch (SE)

Scope: the Settings dialog and the persisted editor preferences (`src/settings.mjs`), the shortcut system (`src/hotkeys.mjs` catalog, dispatcher and `HotkeyManager`; `src/hotkey-settings.mjs` editor; `src/hotkey-hints.mjs`; `src/hotkey-labels.mjs`), the private support/feedback center (`src/support-*.mjs`), telemetry consent and collection (`src/telemetry-*.mjs`, `src/privacy-ui.mjs`), the update notice and the What's new splash (`src/update-ui.mjs`, `src/support-ui.mjs`), localization (`src/localization.mjs`, `src/localization-bindings.mjs`, `src/locales/**`) and the touch interface (`src/touch/*`). Contracts: `docs/hotkeys.md`, `docs/telemetry.md`, `docs/support-service.md`, `docs/UPDATES.md`, `docs/LOCALIZATION.md`, `docs/LOCALIZATION-REVIEW.md`, `docs/TRANSLATING.md`, `docs/TOUCH.md`, `docs/RELEASING.md`. Where code and docs disagree, the code is the truth; differences are in Port notes. The touch interface exists mainly for the browser build on phones and tablets (Electron gets it only when the primary pointer is coarse), so the native port must decide whether to keep a touch layout; the touch rows are separable so each can be accepted or dropped.

The offered set depends on workspace and tool (`MODIFIER_SETS`); a tap under 350 ms latches until tapped again or the tool changes; holding with one thumb while another finger drags is a one-off; the collapsed button shows active names; keyboard/AT click latches; while on, pointer events are re-emitted with modifier keys or the right button; mouse input is untouched. | `src/touch/modifier-dock.mjs` (`MODIFIER_SETS`, `modifiersFor`, `combinedModifiers`, `shapedPointerInit`, `installModifierDock`) | styk-core::stage; styk-platform::input; styk-ui::stage | `tests/touch-gestures.test.mjs`, `tests/touch.browser.mjs` | touch.modifier-dock | todo |

### Screens and states to capture

| Screen ID | States | How to reach it |
|---|---|---|
| settings.general | Closed; open on Interface; tab hover, focus, selected (4 tabs, 5 with Feedback); sidebar Privacy and Donate buttons (default, hover, focus); light and dark; narrow (620px or less, tabs horizontal); phone full-window | Menu StykAnimate > Settings, or Mod+Comma; window 1280x800 and 600x800 |
| settings.search | Empty; matches across several tabs (query "grid"); matches in one group; no results (query "zzzz") | Type in the header search field |
| settings.interface.appearance | Theme tiles (System selected, Light, Dark; hover, focus ring); language select open; partial-language note visible (incomplete pack); UI scale Automatic with note, Custom with slider, Reset enabled and disabled, browser build disabled with note; Arabic/Hebrew mirrored | Settings > Interface |
| settings.interface.startup | Switch on and off | Settings > Interface |
| settings.interface.touch | Touch interface Automatic/On/Off; Draw with a finger options | Settings > Interface |
| settings.interface.timeline | Advanced tile selected, Simple tile selected, hover, focus | Settings > Interface |
| settings.interface.workspace | Reset panel layouts button default and hover | Settings > Interface (host supplies reset) |
| settings.canvas.navigation | Three selects with notes; open select | Settings > Canvas |
| settings.canvas.pasting | Select | Settings > Canvas |
| settings.canvas.duplication | Select with note | Settings > Canvas |
| settings.canvas.handles | Switches on and off; slider at 3, 5, 10; preview strip with and without outline | Settings > Canvas |
| settings.canvas.overlays | Two switches | Settings > Canvas |
| settings.canvas.builder | Switches; colour swatch input; native colour picker open | Settings > Canvas |
| settings.animation.keyframes | Switch on and off | Settings > Animation |
| settings.animation.playback | Quality select open; loop and fit switches | Settings > Animation |
| settings.animation.recovery | Interval select with each preset and a custom value | Settings > Animation |
| settings.shortcuts | Default list grouped by category; search with results; "No shortcuts found"; Reset all with status message; row hover; scrolled | Settings > Shortcuts, or Help > Keyboard shortcuts |
| settings.shortcuts.recording | Binding button pressed ("Press keys"); unassigned command; four bindings (no + button) | Click a binding or + |
| settings.shortcuts.conflict | Conflict alert with Replace and Cancel; status line after replace | Assign a key already used in the scope (e.g. V to Pose tool) |
| settings.shortcuts.gestures | Pen gestures card (5 rows) | Bottom of Shortcuts |
| settings.feedback | Three tiles (Bug, Idea, Other), private-note card with My reports | Settings > Feedback (desktop) |
| settings.error | Alert banner at the top of the content after a failed save | Make the settings write fail (read-only profile) |
| tooltip.shortcut-hint | Tooltip "Name . Shortcut" over a tool; menu row with shortcut badge; unassigned (no badge); rebound key | Hover a tool; open a menu |
| dialog.support | Starting; start failure message; header and body | Settings > Feedback > any tile, or menu Send feedback |
| dialog.support.create | Step 1; step 2 (bug fields shown or hidden by type); Additional details expanded; paused service (send disabled); busy "Sending"; retry-attachments state; validation bubbles | Feedback > New report |
| dialog.support.attachments | Drop zone default, drag-over, disabled; pending card image, video, project; compressing with progress and Cancel; limit notice | Add files in the form or reply |
| dialog.support.inbox | Empty list; list with unread dot and each status pill; load more; narrow width list/detail switch | Feedback > My reports |
| dialog.support.thread | Chip Live/Connecting/Reconnecting/Manual refresh; creator, developer and note messages; sending bubble; closed conversation with locked composer; attachment with preview, download, remove confirm; delete confirm; More menu open | Open a report |
| dialog.support.access | Save/copy access link panel; Open existing inbox form with invalid link error | Inbox access / Other devices disclosure |
| dialog.support.error | Notice strip with each error class | Force service errors |
| dialog.privacy | Default (settings wording); first-run wording | Settings > Privacy, Help > Privacy |
| dialog.privacy.first-run | First launch with no decision | Fresh profile |
| dialog.privacy.custom | Disclosure open with checkbox combinations; status line | "Choose separately" |
| dialog.privacy.data | Data disclosures open; previewed JSON block; export saved or cancelled message; delete message | Inside the dialog |
| dialog.privacy.trace | Dialog closed with recording banner and countdown; after save | "Record 30-second trace" |
| dialog.privacy.error | Error line; buttons disabled while busy | Force failure |
| toast.update | Hidden; checking; downloading with progress; ready (Restart to update, Later); restarting; up to date; unavailable (Open downloads); error (Try again) | Help > Check for updates, or a newer published version |
| dialog.release-notes | Single release; selector with older releases; loading; failed load with Retry; no notes; long notes scroll; light and dark | After an upgrade, or Help > What's new |
| touch.layout | Phone portrait, phone landscape, tablet portrait, tablet landscape; desktop unchanged at 1440x900 | Device emulation or `?ui=touch` |
| touch.tabbar | Workspaces and Properties in the bottom bar | Phone portrait |
| touch.sheet | Bottom sheet at 42%, 62%, 92%; being dragged; side sheet; veil | Open Properties or menus |
| touch.long-press | Tooltip after hold; context menu after hold | Hold a button / a layer row |
| touch.stage | Result of two- and three-finger tap (undo, redo) | Stage on touch |
| touch.modifier-dock | Collapsed; collapsed with active label; expanded per workspace and tool (Animate select, Tweener, Build, Draw brush, Draw eraser); latched buttons; two columns on phone landscape | Stage corner |
| touch.draw | Finger drawing; pen palm rejection | Draw workspace |
| touch.timeline | Fixed playhead mid-grid; zoom at 10 and 72 px | Advanced timeline on touch |
| touch.timeline.actions | Single-frame action bar; range bar with handles; edge fade | Tap Select |
| touch.timeline.simple | Compact cards with hold bar | Simple mode on touch |

### Shortcuts

Every row of `docs/hotkeys.md` and of the catalog in `src/hotkeys.mjs` lands here; the last column names the area that owns the command's behaviour. Mod is Ctrl on Windows/Linux and Cmd on macOS. All catalog bindings are remappable unless stated.

| Keys | Action | Context | Where (code) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mod+N | New animation | Editor | `hotkeys.mjs` `new` (documents area) |
| Mod+O | Open project | Editor | `open` |
| Mod+S | Save project | Editor | `save` |
| Mod+Shift+S | Save project as | Editor | `save-as` |
| Mod+Shift+E | Export | Editor | `export` |
| (unassigned) | Import figures | Editor | `import` |
| Mod+Comma | Settings | Editor | `settings` (this area) |
| Shift+Slash (?) | Keyboard shortcuts (opens Settings > Shortcuts) | Editor | `help` (this area) |
| Mod+P, Mod+Shift+P, F1 | Search commands (command palette) | Editor | `command-palette` |
| Mod+Z | Undo | Editor | `undo` |
| Mod+Shift+Z, Mod+Y | Redo | Editor | `redo` |
| Mod+C / Mod+X / Mod+V | Copy / Cut / Paste selection or frames | Editor | `copy`, `cut`, `paste` |
| Mod+Shift+V | Paste in place | Editor | `paste-in-place` |
| Mod+A | Select all (Drawing scope: Select all pixels) | Editor | `select-all` |
| Mod+D | Duplicate selection (Build: placement of a copy of the selected segment) | Editor | `duplicate` |
| Delete, Backspace | Delete selection / frames | Editor | `delete` |
| Escape | Cancel current gesture (also cancels shortcut recording and duplicate placement) | Editor | `cancel` |
| V | Select tool | Editor | `tool-select` |
| P | Pose tool | Editor | `tool-pose` |
| L | Line tool | Editor | `tool-line` |
| C | Circle tool | Editor | `tool-circle` |
| H | Pan tool | Editor | `tool-hand` |
| (unassigned) | Edit shared rig tool; Polygon tool | Editor | `tool-rig`, `tool-polygon` |
| T | Create text | Editor | `add-text` |
| R (hold) | Hold to edit handles | Editor | `handle-edit-hold` |
| Caps Lock (hold; optional Shift) | Hold to isolate joint | Editor | `isolate-hold` |
| D (hold; optional Shift) | Hold to pose 3D parts in depth | Editor | `depth-hold` |
| (unassigned) | Toggle joint isolation; Rectangle selects figure origins; Edit together | Editor | `toggle-isolate`, `toggle-selection-origins`, `toggle-range-edit` |
| J | Join / unjoin figure | Editor | `join-figure` |
| (unassigned) | Insert copied key at playhead | Editor | `insert-copied-key` |
| F7 | Insert blank key at playhead | Editor | `insert-blank-key` |
| Space (hold to pan) | Play / pause; holding and dragging pans | Editor | `play-pan` |
| (unassigned) | Play / pause immediately | Editor | `play` |
| A | Add Frame (copy of the pose after the current frame; both timeline modes) | Editor | `add-frame` |
| Shift+A | Extend keyframe | Editor | `extend-keyframe` |
| (unassigned) | Delete frames | Editor | `delete-frame` |
| Comma / Period (repeat) | Previous / next frame | Editor | `previous-frame`, `next-frame` |
| Shift+Comma / Shift+Period (repeat) | Jump to previous / next keyframe | Editor | `previous-keyframe`, `next-keyframe` |
| Home / End | First / last frame | Editor | `first-frame`, `last-frame` |
| (unassigned) | Toggle playback loop; Follow selected figures with the camera | Editor | `toggle-loop`, `camera-follow` |
| O | Toggle onion skin | Editor | `toggle-onion` |
| K | Toggle auto key | Editor | `toggle-auto-key` |
| Shift+F5 | Remove frames | Advanced timeline focused | `remove-frames` |
| Shift+F6 | Remove keyframes | Advanced timeline focused | `clear-keys` |
| Up / Down (repeat; optional Shift) | Select layer above / below | Advanced timeline focused | `timeline-layer-up`, `timeline-layer-down` |
| (unassigned; repeat; optional Shift) | Previous / next exposure | Simple timeline mode | `previous-exposure`, `next-exposure` |
| = or Shift+=, Minus | Zoom in / Zoom out | Editor | `zoom-in`, `zoom-out` |
| Shift+1 / Shift+2 | Fit canvas / Fit selection | Editor | `zoom-reset`, `zoom-selection` |
| Shift+J | Show / hide joints | Editor | `toggle-handles` |
| M | Show / hide motion paths (in Draw: flip view) | Editor | `toggle-motion-paths`, `drawing-flip-view` |
| Mod+' | Show / hide grid | Editor | `toggle-grid` |
| Mod+H | Show / hide unselected handles | Editor | `toggle-unselected-handles` |
| Mod+K | Show / hide camera | Editor | `toggle-camera` |
| Mod+Alt+K | Reset camera | Editor | `camera-reset` |
| F8 / F10 / F11 / F12 | Center camera / Align camera to view / Copy camera / Paste camera (F10 and F12 are reused in Build) | Editor | `camera-center`, `camera-align-view`, `camera-copy`, `camera-paste` |
| Shift+H / Shift+V | Flip horizontally / vertically (Build: flip the selected segment) | Editor | `mirror`, `mirror-vertical` |
| I | Segment editor | Canvas focused | `segment-values` |
| Arrow keys (hold, repeat; Shift = align) | Nudge handle left / right / up / down | Canvas focused | `handle-left/right/up/down` |
| Mod+Left / Mod+Right (repeat) | Select previous / next figure | Canvas focused | `previous-figure`, `next-figure` |
| Alt+Arrow (repeat; Shift = 10 px) | Move selection | Editor | `move-left/right/up/down` |
| L / Shift+L | Locate handle in previous / next frame | Handle hovered | `locate-previous`, `locate-next` |
| S (optional Shift) | Split segment / branch; Shift+S includes the branch | Figure builder | `split-segment` |
| B | Toggle branch scope | Figure builder | `toggle-builder-branch` |
| F9 / F10 / F12 | Construction background colour / Show static handles / Highlight selected segment | Figure builder | `builder-canvas-color`, `builder-static-handles`, `builder-highlight` |
| Mod+Delete, Mod+Backspace | Remove joint, keep endpoints | Figure builder | `unsplit-segment` |
| Shift+Delete, Shift+Backspace | Delete branch | Figure builder | `delete-segment` |
| Enter | Finish polygon | Drawing polygon | `finish-polygon` |
| B / E / G / L / U | Brush / Eraser / Fill / Select pixels / Shapes | Drawing workspace | `drawing-brush/eraser/fill/lasso/shape` |
| [ and ] (repeat); Shift+[ and Shift+] (repeat) | Smaller / larger brush; softer / harder brush | Drawing workspace | `drawing-smaller/larger/softer/harder` |
| 1 to 9, 0 | Brush opacity 10% to 90%, 100% | Drawing workspace | `drawing-opacity-10..100` |
| Enter | Apply pixel selection | Drawing workspace | `drawing-apply` |
| Mod+A / Mod+Shift+A / Mod+Shift+I | Select all / Deselect / Invert pixel selection | Drawing workspace | `drawing-select-all`, `drawing-deselect`, `drawing-invert-selection` |
| Shift+Backspace | Fill pixel selection | Drawing workspace | `drawing-fill-selection` |
| Shift+M / X / M | Toggle mirror painting / Swap drawing colours / Flip view horizontally | Drawing workspace | `drawing-mirror`, `drawing-swap-colors`, `drawing-flip-view` |
| Enter / F2 | Open or insert library item / Rename library item | Library focused | `library-open`, `library-rename` |
| Mod+W | Close project | Desktop only | `close-project` |
| Mod+Tab (Ctrl+Tab on macOS; repeat) | Last used project | Desktop only | `recent-project` |
| Mod+PageDown / Mod+PageUp | Next / previous project | Desktop only | `next-project`, `previous-project` |
| (unassigned) | Save all projects | Editor | `save-all` |
| Mod+1 / 2 / 3 / 4 | Show or fold Properties / VFX / Library / timeline (focuses it; folds when it already has focus) | Desktop only | `panel-toggle-*` |
| Mod+Backslash | Focus mode (panels fold to strips; again to restore) | Editor | `layout-focus` |
| Mod+Alt+M | Move the focused panel (arrows choose Dock left / Dock right / Float, Enter moves, Escape cancels; works in text fields) | Editor | `layout-arrange` |
| Mod+Alt+L | Layout... (View > Layout presets and positions; works in text fields) | Editor | `layout-menu` |
| F6 / Mod+F6 | Next / previous region (top band, tools, side panels, stage, timeline); works from text fields | Editor | `focus-next-region`, `focus-previous-region` |
| Shift+F10 or Menu key | Open the focused panel tab's menu (Dock left, Dock right, Float, Fold to strip, Hide) | Focused panel tab | `src/shell/dock-keyboard.mjs` (layout area) |
| Arrow keys (Shift = bigger steps), End, Home or Enter | Panel splitter: resize, largest size, default size | Focused splitter | `src/shell/dock-keyboard.mjs` (layout area) |
| Arrow keys, Home, End | Move between Settings tabs | Settings tablist | `settings.mjs` |
| Escape | Clear Settings search, then close Settings | Settings search | `settings.mjs` |
| Escape / Tab | Cancel shortcut recording (Tab keeps focus navigation) | Shortcut recording | `hotkey-settings.mjs` |
| Ctrl/Cmd+Enter | Send support reply | Support composer | `support-center.mjs` |
| Escape | Close privacy, support, What's new dialogs | Modal dialog | `privacy-ui.mjs`, `support-desktop-ui.mjs`, `support-ui.mjs` |
| Shift-drag a figure origin; Ctrl/Cmd-drag | Carry pinned IK nodes; constrain origin move to one axis (combinable; motion-path origins use the same; other trail points keep Shift for axis lock). Pointer modifiers, not remappable. | Animate stage | `docs/hotkeys.md` (stage area owns) |
| Pen barrel button, eraser end, Space+drag, Ctrl+Space+drag, Shift+Space+drag | Pen and pan gestures listed (read-only) in Settings > Shortcuts | Stage | `settings.mjs` (list); stage area implements |
| Two-finger tap / three-finger tap / press and hold / double tap | Undo / Redo / context menu / double-click | Stage on touch | `canvas-touch.mjs` |

### Platform features used

| Feature | Where | Native owner |
|---|---|---|
| localStorage (`stykanimate.editor.settings.v1`, browser only) and host IPC `getEditorSettings` / `setEditorSettings` | `src/settings.mjs`; `desktop/preload.cjs` | styk-platform::storage |
| `<dialog>` modal (showModal, focus trap, ::backdrop, Escape, close event) for Settings, Support, Privacy, What's new | all dialogs | styk-ui::settings; styk-ui::dialogs |
| `matchMedia` ((max-width:620px), (pointer: coarse), prefers-color-scheme), resize and orientationchange | `src/settings.mjs`; `src/touch/touch-mode.mjs`; `src/theme.mjs` | styk-platform::input; styk-platform::shell |
| Host `setUIScale` (zoom factor) and `screen.width/height` | `src/settings.mjs`, `src/ui-scale.mjs` | styk-platform::shell |
| Keyboard events (`KeyboardEvent.code`, `isComposing`, `getModifierState('AltGraph')`, capture-phase keydown/keyup, key repeat) | `src/hotkeys.mjs`, `src/hotkey-settings.mjs` | styk-platform::input |
| Form controls and ARIA roles (range, color, search, switch, radio, select, details, tablist, radiogroup, log, status, alert) | `src/settings.mjs`, `src/support-center.mjs` | styk-design::widgets |
| Intl (NumberFormat, DateTimeFormat, ListFormat, PluralRules, Locale, getCanonicalLocales) | `src/localization.mjs` | styk-core::i18n |
| Dynamic `import()` of one language module | `src/localization.mjs` (`loadLocale`) | styk-core::i18n |
| HTML `lang` / `dir`, bidi isolation marks | `src/localization-bindings.mjs` | styk-ui::text |
| fetch with `AbortSignal.timeout`, WebSocket (wss) | `src/support-center.mjs`, `src/support-live-client.mjs` | styk-platform::network |
| Host IPC for support (`supportRequest`, `getSupportContext`, `saveSupportState`, `connectSupportLive`, `onSupportLive`, `copySupportAccess`, `restoreSupportAccess`, `downloadSupportFile`) | `src/support-desktop-ui.mjs` | styk-platform::support |
| Drag and drop of files onto the attachment zone; `<input type=file multiple accept>` | `src/support-center.mjs` | styk-platform::files |
| `createImageBitmap`, `OffscreenCanvas.convertToBlob` (WebP), `<video>`, `captureStream`, `MediaRecorder` (VP8/Opus WebM), object URLs | `src/support-media.mjs` | styk-platform::image; styk-platform::video |
| `crypto.randomUUID`, `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, `beforeunload` | `src/support-center.mjs` | styk-platform::clipboard; styk-platform::shell |
| `PerformanceObserver` (longtask, event, long-animation-frame), `performance.memory`, `document.hasFocus`, `visibilitychange`, `setInterval` | `src/telemetry-core.mjs`, `src/telemetry-performance.mjs` | styk-platform::telemetry |
| Host IPC for telemetry (`getTelemetryState`, `onTelemetryState`, `setTelemetryConsent`, `recordTelemetry`, `previewTelemetry`, `exportTelemetry`, `deleteTelemetry`, `recordPerformanceTrace`, `sendTelemetryFeedback`) | `src/privacy-ui.mjs`, `src/telemetry-core.mjs` | styk-platform::telemetry |
| Chromium tracing (30 s trace capture) | host `recordPerformanceTrace` | styk-devtools |
| Host IPC for updates (`getUpdateState`, `onUpdateState`, `checkForUpdates`, `openUpdateReleases`, `getReleaseInfo`, `getReleaseHistory`, `openDonate`, restart flow); electron-updater, NSIS and AppImage replacement | `src/update-ui.mjs`, `src/support-ui.mjs`, `desktop/` | styk-platform::updates |
| `<progress>` and `aria-live` regions | `src/update-ui.mjs` | styk-design::widgets |
| Custom protocol `stykanimate://open` and `stykanimate://privacy` | `desktop/main.cjs` | styk-platform::shell |
| Pointer Events (pointerType, isPrimary, pointer capture, `touchmove` preventDefault, Safari `gesture*`), `navigator.vibrate`, `elementFromPoint`, synthetic `contextmenu` and PointerEvent dispatch | `src/touch/*` | styk-platform::input |
| CSS `env(safe-area-inset-*)`, `touch-action`, `overscroll-behavior`, `[data-input]` styling, `backdrop-filter` (privacy backdrop blur 3px) | `src/styles/layout/touch.css`, `src/styles/features/touch.css`, `src/privacy-ui.mjs` | styk-design::tokens |
| ResizeObserver (tab bar size) | `src/touch/touch-shell.mjs` | styk-ui::shell |
| Cloudflare Worker telemetry and support service (HTTPS only, site origin from `desktop/service-config.json`) | `docs/telemetry.md`, `docs/support-service.md` | styk-platform::network |

### Port notes

- Code versus docs: `docs/hotkeys.md` has a heading "Architecture" placed before the pointer-modifier and Build-shortcut prose, so the doc's structure is misleading; the contract is the catalog in `src/hotkeys.mjs`. The doc refers to "Preferences -> Privacy & diagnostics"; the code labels it with the sidebar Privacy button (`settings.privacy`) plus Help. `docs/UPDATES.md` calls Linux AppImage auto-update new in Unreleased, but `desktop/appimage-updater.cjs` already exists. The doc's "splash waits for the first frame and open dialogs" is implemented by `queueStartupPrompts` in `src/app.mjs` (release notes priority 2; privacy first-run priority 1 but ready only after the release info is known).
- Settings fields and defaults that must survive with the same clamping: locale en; theme system; uiScale 100 (75-200, step 5); uiScaleMode auto; touchInterface auto; fingerDraw auto; hotkeys = catalog defaults; timelineMode advanced; replaceKeyframesOnDrop true; showHandles true; showCamera false; showUnselectedHandles true; canvasGrid false; canvasZoomGesture ctrl-scroll; smoothZoom auto; marqueeSelection origins; pasteDepth same; altDragDuplicate instance; canvasGuideVertical/Horizontal/Locked false; canvasGridSpacing 40 (4-1000); canvasGridOpacity 0.18 (0.05-0.6); canvasGuideX 0.5 and canvasGuideY 0.75 (0-1); motionPaths false (session only); motionPathRange 0 (0-4000); motionPathLabels true; motionPathDots true; motionPathAllTargets false; motionPathJoints selected (selected/tips); motionPathSpeed false; motionPathRelative false; motionPathFalloffRadius 8 (1-240); motionPathFalloffShape smooth (smooth/linear/sharp/flat); autoSizeHandles true; outlineHandles true; builderShowStaticHandles true; builderSmartSnapping true; builderHighlightSegment false; builderCanvasColor #ffffff; useBuilderCanvasColor false; builderOutlinerWidth 236 (160-520); handleSize 5 (3-10); onionCount 0 (0-9); onionAhead true; onionJoined true; onionOpacity 0.14 (0-1); onionBefore 2; onionAfter 1; onionRangeBefore/After null (0 to 10,000,000); onionStep 1 (1-20); onionFade true; onionTint true; onionBeforeColor #e76b70; onionAfterColor #549de7; onionScope all (all/selected); onionMode keyframes (keyframes/frames); onionCamera false; onionShowOnTop false; onionTimelineMarkers true; autoKey false; loopPlayback true; fitViewOnPlayback true; playbackQuality normal; autosaveSeconds 2.5 (0-300); inspectorSections {}; exportPreferences null; showHomeOnStartup true; homeLayout grid (grid/list); homeSort recent (recent/name/modified); tutorialsDone [] (ids matching `[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,39}`, max 64). Native storage should be a versioned file with the same normalization and a one-time import of the existing profile JSON. `smartSnapping` (`builderSmartSnapping`) is persisted but has no Settings control.
- Settings apply live (no OK button). The patch reaches the app first and is persisted 200 ms later (immediately for a language change); persistence must not block the interface thread. The retry-on-next-close and visible-error behaviour is a deliberate contract. The onionOpacity control, where one exists, shows percent and stores a fraction.
- The Settings search is built by rendering all searchable tabs into a detached template and filtering DOM rows by text. In a native UI keep one declarative description of each setting (id, tab, group, label, hint, control kind) and generate the pages, the search and the palette index from it, or the index will drift.
- Hotkeys are physical key codes (layout independent): `Shift+Slash` (the ? key on US layouts) and `Equal` / `Shift+Equal` for zoom depend on that, so bind to physical keys, not produced characters. AltGraph and IME composition must never trigger commands.
- Catalog keys are deliberately reused across scopes: B (brush in Drawing, branch scope in Build), L (Line tool global, locate handle on hover, Select pixels in Drawing), M (motion paths global, flip view in Drawing), F10 and F12 (camera align / paste global, static handles / highlight in Build), Enter (finish polygon, open library item, apply pixel selection), Mod+A. Conflicts are checked only inside one scope; dispatch tries the most specific active scopes first, then global.
- `extraShift` commands must report whether Shift was physically held even though it is not part of the binding (used for align, 10 px, include branch). Exact bindings beat the Shift variant.
- Hold commands need key-up delivered even when a control would swallow it, and every hold must release on focus loss, dialog open, rebinding and window hide. Space is `play-pan`: a tap toggles play, a hold with drag pans.
- Hints are generated from the live binding map; a control must never carry both a native tooltip and the app tooltip; a key claimed by the Draw workspace shows Draw's command.
- Timings and limits to preserve: settings save debounce 200 ms; telemetry tick 15 s; "active" window 60 s; upload at most every 15 minutes; seven-day offline queue; 32 KiB report ceiling; 30 s local trace; support live coalescing 150 ms, handshake 10 s, backoff 1 s doubling to 30 s plus up to 0.5 s jitter, 5 attempts; support request timeouts 20 s / 90 s; update check 10 s after launch then hourly.
- Telemetry contract is server-facing: schema version 3 and policy version `2026-09-10` with allowlists in `src/telemetry-schema.mjs` are validated by the live Cloudflare Worker. A native collector either emits the same JSON or bumps the schema deliberately. Nothing may run without consent: Reject and closing the first-run prompt both mean off; disabled means no timers, observers or files. Withdrawal aborts uploads; deletion rotates the identity; the token is never in a preview or export. Browser-only sources (PerformanceObserver event / long-animation-frame, `performance.memory`) have no native equivalent, so decide which metrics survive and keep their names stable for the dashboard.
- Support: the inbox is identified by a 64-hex access key, not an account; drafts are keyed by inbox id; attachment preparation is local and sequential and must never upload originals as a fallback. Limits: 6 files and 24 MB per thread, 8 MB per file (4 MB images after compression), message 1-4000, title 5-120, description 10-4000, retention 90 days (reports) and 30 days (files), page size 30. Admin mode inside `support-center.mjs` (moderation notes prefixed `Moderation: `) belongs to the website; only user mode ships in the app.
- Updates: the state machine idle, checking, downloading, ready, restarting plus unavailable, current and error is read by the notice and its tests; keep the names. Restart must flush settings and clipboard persistence and run the save-before-restart decision; a failed or cancelled save never launches the installer. Portable, development and read-only copies only explain and link to downloads. The installed version's notes come from a bundled payload (works offline), older releases are fetched on demand (stable only, max 20, sorted by version descending); the Ko-fi link opens in the system browser.
- Localization: lookup is by stable semantic key with English fallback per key and per plural category; use a CLDR-aware plural implementation in Rust (Arabic has six forms) and keep the `{name}` placeholder syntax and nested `{messageKey, messageParams}` references and the number/date/list descriptors. Never translate shortcuts, ids, filenames, extensions, authored figure names or animation text. Keep the placeholder-parity validator (`locales:check`) in CI. The pack JSON files can be reused as-is; the generated `messages/*.mjs` modules cannot.
- Right-to-left is a whole-layout feature (Arabic, Hebrew): iced needs bidi text shaping and mirrored layout, with timeline, canvas, numeric inputs and shortcut badges kept left-to-right. A language change must keep focus, drafts, selection and scroll in open dialogs, and playback must not trigger per-frame text updates.
- Touch constants a porter would get wrong: hold 480 ms; hold slop 10 px (multi-finger tap slop 15 px); double tap 320 ms and 28 px; multi-finger tap 360 ms; modifier latch tap under 350 ms; click swallow 400 ms; haptic 8 ms; hold tooltip 1.6 s; sheet detents 42/62/92% (close under 30%); side-sheet close 72 px; sheet drag slop 8 px; range handle reach 24 px; touch frame width 30 (10-72) px; rows 48 px (Camera, Scene and ruler 44 px); momentum friction 0.93; phone when the shorter side is under 600 px; tablet stage minimum 560 px; touch targets 44 px phone / 40 px tablet; upright phone timeline 47% of height; palm rejection 700 ms.
- Touch re-emits events instead of sharing a model: the modifier dock stops the trusted event at the viewport and sends a copy with modifier keys or the right button; the touch timeline sends the grid an untrusted copy of the press. A native input model should carry explicit modifier state so tools have one code path. Decision needed: keep a touch shell for Windows tablets, or support pen, touch, pinch and palm rejection inside the desktop layout only.
- Cross-references only (no rows here): the command palette and menu items Help > Keyboard shortcuts, Privacy, Check for updates, What's new, Send feedback (shell area); dock keyboard and `src/timeline-hotkeys.mjs` (layout and timeline areas); Home and tutorials settings UIs (home area); export preferences UI (export area); onion, grid, guide and motion-path controls that use the persisted values in SE-028 (stage, timeline and camera areas); recovery itself (documents area).

## Desktop main process, shell, web build and platform features (DK)

Scope: everything the Electron main process does for the interface (`desktop/main.cjs` registers 100+ `ipcMain.handle` channels, 1 `ipcMain.on` sync channel and 8 push channels; `desktop/preload.cjs` exposes them as `window.stykDesktop`), the services behind them (`desktop/*.cjs`), the packaging contract (`package.json` `build`, `desktop/installer.nsh`, `scripts/before-pack.cjs`), the browser build (`src/web-offline.mjs`, `src/web-support.js`, `src/codec-web-*.mjs`, `src/browser-recovery.mjs`, `src/web-library.mjs`, `src/community-library.mjs`), every browser API the interface relies on (swept across `src/`), and every diagnostic the app has (environment variables, privacy and telemetry panes, the Performance dialog, the performance trace, the support center, crash records). Contracts are stated in `docs/DESKTOP-RELEASE.md`, `docs/LINUX.md`, `docs/WEB.md`, `docs/UPDATES.md`, `docs/APP-START.md`, `desktop/README.md`, `desktop/ACCELERATION.md` and the desktop sections of `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`. Where the code and a document disagree the code wins; the differences are listed in Port notes. Features owned by other areas (the project tab strip, the Home grid, the Library panel UI, export dialogs, the timeline, the stage renderer, hotkey recording) have no row here; the rows cover only their platform side (IPC, file, dialog, storage).

Conventions: `win` = Windows, `mac` = macOS, `linux` = Linux. "Renderer" = the Electron page. Every IPC channel is `styk:<name>`; each handler first calls `trusted(event)`, which refuses any sender that is not the one editor window on the `styk-app://app` origin. In the native app there is no renderer, so the owner column names where the same behaviour lands.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DK-001 | Single application instance. The first launch takes the single-instance lock and owns the profile. A later ordinary launch is NOT refused: it runs as its own process and window with its own session and recovery directory (`workspaces/<pid>`). A later launch that carries a `stykanimate://` link while no primary exists quits; with a primary the link is forwarded to the primary. | `desktop/main.cjs` (`requestSingleInstanceLock`, `second-instance`, `instanceDirectory`); `desktop/workspace-instance.cjs` (`workspaceDirectory`, `processAlive`) | styk-platform::shell | `scripts/workspace-instance.test.mjs` | | todo |

### Screens and states to capture

| Screen ID | States | How to reach it |
|---|---|---|
| global.window | default (frameless, Win/Linux), maximized, fullscreen, unfocused; macOS hidden-inset title bar with traffic lights and 84 px left padding | Launch the desktop app at 1560x1000; maximize through the controls; macOS capture needs a Mac |
| global.window-controls | default, hover (each of minimize, maximize, close), pressed, focus ring, window maximized (restore glyph), hidden on macOS | Top band at right; Windows or Linux build |
| menu.native-bar | closed, open File, Edit, Animation, View, Help (Alt on Win/Linux), items with no shortcut labels, View without Developer tools in the packaged app, translated labels | Press Alt; Help items include Check for updates and What's new |
| dialog.os-open | default with filter list, cancelled | File > Open project (Ctrl+O) |
| dialog.os-save | default (project .styk filter, Untitled.styk), overwrite confirmation, export names (video.webm, avi), cancelled | Ctrl+S on a new project; Export > Video then Save video |
| dialog.unsaved-close | one project ("Save changes to X?"), several projects, buttons Save / Cancel / Discard | Edit anything, then close the window; open two projects with edits for the plural form |
| dialog.unsaved-update | one project, several projects (Save and restart / Cancel / Discard and restart) | Needs an update in `ready` state (use `scripts/smoke-updates.mjs`) then Restart to update with unsaved work |
| dialog.editor-stopped | default error box with three buttons | Force a renderer crash (kill the renderer process) |
| global.update-notice | hidden, checking, downloading (progress bar, percent, MB), ready (Restart to update, Later), restarting, up to date, unavailable (Open downloads), error (Try again, Dismiss), narrow window | Help > Check for updates; states are driven by the update bridge in `scripts/smoke-updates.mjs` |
| dialog.release-notes | default (installed version notes grouped by category), version selector open, history loading, history failed (Retry), empty notes, long notes scrolled, light and dark | First start of a new version, or Help > What's new |
| dialog.privacy | first-run (intro text), regular, details open (checkboxes), saving message, preview JSON shown, error line, recording banner | Start with a fresh profile (STYK_USER_DATA_DIR empty) or Settings > Privacy |
| settings.privacy.trace | idle button, "Recording" button state, top banner "Recording locally, N s remaining" | Privacy dialog > Record a performance trace |
| dialog.performance | default with samples, before playback (dashes), activity list with an error row, Copy report > Copied, Could not copy | Help > Performance after playing a scene |
| dialog.support | create form, inbox list, thread, live connection unavailable, attachment saving, drafts restored, access link restore | Help > Report a problem; needs the service reachable (fixture in `tests/support`) |
| dialog.feedback | default, too-short message error, sent | Privacy dialog > Report a problem or share an idea |
| dialog.recent-files | list with entries, entry marked unavailable (disabled), empty, after Clear history | File > Open recent (Home: recent) |
| dialog.recovery | single copy offer, several copies with Manage, restoring, discard confirmation | Kill the app with unsaved work, relaunch |
| home.projects | project cards (pinned, folder, missing file, open marker), folder row, empty list | Home view with several saved projects |
| home.projects.card | thumbnail present, no thumbnail, stats line, missing | Home card |
| menu.home-project | pin, move to folder, show in folder, remove | Right-click or menu on a Home card |
| panel.library | folders and items, thumbnails loading, empty folder, save conflict, error toast | Library panel > My library |
| panel.library.community | catalogue loaded, categories, preview loading, offline error, asset removed, download verified | Library panel > Community; needs network or the stand-in site |
| menu.library-item | rename, move, show in folder, delete | Right-click a library item |
| dialog.text.installed-fonts | font list loading, list, none installed | Text tool dialog > Installed fonts |
| dialog.export.video | codec list with unsupported reasons, odd-size padding notice, audio option disabled reason | Export > Video (desktop) |
| dialog.export.video.vfw | codec list x86 and x64, unavailable reason, configure button | Export > Video > Windows codecs (Windows only) |
| dialog.os-codec-config | the codec's own configuration dialog owned by the main window | Windows codec Configure button |
| timeline.audio | imported audio waveform, decoding progress, decode error, size limit error | Timeline > Add audio |
| global.drop-overlay | hidden, visible while dragging files over the window | Drag a file from the file manager over the window |
| settings.recovery | autosave interval select (Off, presets, custom value) | Settings > Recovery |
| settings.interface | UI scale auto/manual slider 75-200 | Settings > Interface |
| toast.web-update | "A new version of StykAnimate is ready" for 10 s | Web build after deploying a new version |
| toast.codec-loading | "converter is loading" status on first Pivot file | Web build, open a .piv |
| toast.recovery-unavailable | blocked or full storage notice | Web build with storage blocked |
| web.unsupported | missing-feature page | Open the web build in a browser without OffscreenCanvas (test with `tests/web-support.browser.mjs`) |

### Shortcuts

| Keys | Action | Context | Where (code) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mod+N | New animation | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`new`) |
| Mod+O | Open project (native dialog) | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`open`) |
| Mod+S | Save project (streamed, atomic) | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`save`) |
| Mod+Shift+S | Save project as | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`save-as`) |
| Mod+Shift+E | Export | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`export`) |
| Mod+Comma | Settings | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`settings`) |
| Shift+Slash (?) | Keyboard shortcuts | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`help`) |
| Mod+P, Mod+Shift+P, F1 | Search commands (command palette) | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`command-palette`) |
| Mod+W | Close project | desktop app only (a browser keeps it) | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`DESKTOP_PROJECT_KEYS`) |
| Mod+Tab (macOS: Ctrl+Tab) | Last used project (repeats) | desktop app only | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`DESKTOP_PROJECT_KEYS`) |
| Mod+PageDown / Mod+PageUp | Next / previous project | desktop app only | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`DESKTOP_PROJECT_KEYS`) |
| Mod+1, Mod+2, Mod+3, Mod+4 | Show or fold Properties, VFX, Library, timeline | desktop app only | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`DESKTOP_PANEL_KEYS`) |
| F6 / Mod+F6 | Next / previous region of the editor (works from a text field) | global | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`focus-next-region`, `focus-previous-region`); `src/app.mjs` (`SHELL_FIELD_KEYS`) |
| Alt | Show the hidden native menu bar | Windows and Linux desktop | `desktop/main.cjs` (`autoHideMenuBar`) |
| (none) | Native menu items carry no accelerators by design; labels only | desktop | `desktop/main.cjs` (`nativeMenu`) |
| Cmd+Q, Cmd+H, Cmd+M etc. | macOS standard app menu roles | macOS | `desktop/main.cjs` (`{ role: 'appMenu' }`) |
| Alt+F4 / window close | Close window with unsaved-work prompt | Windows, Linux | `desktop/main.cjs` (`window.on('close')`) |
| Mod+Alt+L, Mod+Alt+M, Mod+Backslash | Layout menu, move focused panel, focus mode | global (owned by layout area; listed for the `Mod` rule) | `src/hotkeys.mjs` |
| Mouse: drag files in | Import dropped files | whole window | `src/app.mjs` (`drop`) |
| Mouse: Enter / F2 | Open or insert library item; rename library item | Library panel focused | `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`library-open`, `library-rename`) |

### Port notes

- Channel table (all `styk:` prefixed). Updates: update-state, update-check, update-releases, update-restart, release-info, release-history, donate. Support: support-context, support-live, support-live-close, support-save-state, support-request, support-copy-access, support-restore, support-download. Telemetry: telemetry-state, telemetry-consent, telemetry-record, telemetry-preview, telemetry-sync, telemetry-delete, telemetry-feedback, telemetry-export, service-page, performance-trace. Library: library-list, -mkdir, -thumbnail, -save-begin, -write, -finish, -cancel, -read-begin, -read, -change, -reveal, -trash, -thumbnail-cache. Public library: public-library-list, -preview, -read, -save, -page, -link, plus page-listening. Recent and projects: recent-list, -open, -clear; projects-list, -open, -update, -remove, -reveal, -preview, -preview-set, -folder. Clipboard: clipboard-text-read, -write, shared-clipboard-write-begin, -write, -finish, -read-begin, -read, -cancel. Audio: audio-begin, -write, -finish, -read, -cancel. Video: video-capabilities, -begin, -frame-begin, -frame-chunk, -frame-end, -finish, -read, -cancel, -audio-chunk, -save. VFW: the same set under vfw- plus vfw-configure. Documents and files: document-activate, -select, -release, -state, project-write-begin, file-write-begin, -chunk, -patch, -finish, -cancel, recovery-write-begin, recovery-info, -list, -read-begin, -save, -get, -delete, file-read-chunk, file-read-close, open-file, save-file. Window: window, window-state, new-window. Preferences: workspace-preferences-get/set, editor-settings-get/set, vfx-presets-get/set, fonts-list, fonts-read, samples-list, sample-read, memory-bytes (sync). Pushes: menu-action, file-opened, window-state, update-state-changed, telemetry-state-changed, support-live-event, public-library-link.
- The renderer never sees a path. Every path-bearing feature uses opaque ids or tokens (recent ids are UUIDs, project ids, document tokens, open tokens, font ids `font_<32 hex>`, recovery slots). Keep that in the native app's public API so scripts and tests can still drive it; the application core can hold real paths internally but the automation surface should keep the ids.
- Save semantics worth keeping exactly: `.styk` is the only saveable project type (Pivot, Stick Nodes and the rest are import or export); Save with no target behaves like Save as; the save target changes only after the file is committed; a document switch cancels in-flight transfers; a failed or cancelled write leaves the old file; recovery copies are separate files per open project and never listed while their owner is open.
- Rename-retry delays are exactly 20, 40, 80, 160, 320, 400 ms on EPERM, EACCES and EBUSY (antivirus and indexers on Windows). The native saver needs the same retry.
- Timings: update check 10 s after start then hourly (unref'd); telemetry upload timer 60 s but a batch at most every 15 minutes plus up to 60 s jitter; telemetry tick in the page 15 s; telemetry main memory sample 60 s while focused; file transfer idle timeout 120 s; shared clipboard transfer expiry 120 s; audio import timeout 120 s; font scan once then cached; release history cache 5 min; community catalogue cache 10 min; VFW capability cache 10 s; web service worker registers 3 s after load; web update toast 10 s; idle codec worker stops after 2 s; GPU recovery retries 1 s then 5 s.
- Limits table for tests: files 256 MiB (sized), 16 GiB (streamed), 8 concurrent transfers, 4 MiB chunk (the renderer sends 256 KiB; the error text says "256KB" but the code limit is 4 MiB); recovery 256 MiB, 16 copies; workspace preferences 16 KiB, VFX presets 1 MiB, recents 512 KiB and 20 entries, projects 200 and 1 MiB, folders 48; video 16384 px, 16 Mpx, 1 GiB output, 1000 fps; VFW 67 Mpx, 256 MiB output; audio 32 MiB input, one hour; library names 120 chars; clipboard text 100 chars; shared clipboard 256 MiB, 4 transfers; support attachment 8 MiB, drafts 12.
- Documents that disagree with the code: (1) `desktop/README.md` says `STYK_DEBUG=1` prints codec process logs; no code reads `STYK_DEBUG` (the codec is WebAssembly in a page worker). (2) `desktop/README.md` describes recovery as `saveRecovery({data base64})` and "changed loopback origin"; the page now uses streamed per-project recovery and a fixed `styk-app://app` origin, and the base64 API remains only for compatibility. (3) `docs/UPDATES.md` calls Linux AppImage auto-update "new in Unreleased"; the code ships it (`desktop/appimage-updater.cjs`) and `release-info.json` 1.2.5 lists it. (4) `desktop/README.md` says Node 22; the release workflow pins 24.11.0. (5) The file-streams chunk error text says 256 KB but the enforced limit is 4 MiB.
- Second launches are separate processes by design: each owns its project, recovery directory and window. The single-instance lock only decides who handles `stykanimate://` links. A native app with true multiple windows in one process must preserve "each window owns its documents and its recovery directory" and the rule that a protocol link goes to the primary window only.
- Frameless window with custom controls is Windows and Linux only; macOS keeps native traffic lights. The title-bar drag region and double-click maximize live in the top band (owned by the shell area).
- The native menu bar is deliberately accelerator-free so the rebindable hotkey system (which respects focus, IME and disabled commands) is the only key handler. Keep any native menu for macOS or accessibility, but never attach accelerators.
- `trusted(event)` and the `will-navigate`, permission and `window.open` lockdown exist only because the UI is web content; the native app has no equivalent surface, but the allowlist of external URLs (github.com, pivotanimate.net for window.open; fixed Ko-fi, releases, site pages) should be kept for any link the interface opens.
- Errors crossing IPC carry `messageKey` and `messageParams` and are translated in the main process with the current locale (`handle` wrapper in `main.cjs`). Native code should keep typed errors with keys, because dialogs built from them must follow the interface language and update when the language changes.
- Telemetry schema, allowlists and privacy text are shared JavaScript (`src/telemetry-schema.mjs` is loaded by the main process from the web resources). The native app has to port the schema validation (day snapshots, histograms, fingerprints, operation contexts) or telemetry will not validate against the existing server.
- Support center and privacy dialog are web forms inside `<dialog>`; the native text-input requirement includes multiline fields with drafts saved while typing (support-drafts save is debounced by the support center and serialized in main).
- Update restart ordering matters: stage the Linux copy first, ask about unsaved work, flush telemetry, then install; the "revision" checkpoint prevents installing over edits made while the question box was open.
- The community library asks the site's counting route only for the first download of an item per installation (`community-downloads.json`, 5000 ids, oldest dropped); losing that file makes the site count again.
- Library items are three files (`name.stkz|stykmov`, `name.png`, `name.json` for the `set` kind); rename, move and trash must move all three, and listing ignores dotfiles and symlinks.
- Fonts: TTC/OTC faces are rebuilt as standalone fonts (collection offsets are absolute); the list is name-records only so a 20000-file scan stays bounded; the native build can use the OS font API, but must still expose stable opaque ids and a bounded cache.
- Browser-only paths to drop from the native build: service worker and web manifest (DK-105), web-support check (DK-104), `beforeunload` (DK-055), IndexedDB recovery and library (DK-053, DK-069), codec worker host (DK-106), web video export (DK-083), `/community` proxy (DK-068). They still ship as long as the browser build ships.
- Not found in `src/`: `sessionStorage`, `BroadcastChannel`, `SharedWorker`, `AudioWorklet`, `VideoDecoder`, `showOpenFilePicker`, `Notification`, `EyeDropper`, `wakeLock`, `speechSynthesis`, `ClipboardItem`, `requestFullscreen`; do not reproduce them. Fullscreen is the Electron `togglefullscreen` menu role only.
- Cross-references (owned elsewhere): project tab strip and project switcher (`src/shell/project-tabs-view.mjs`), Home view and tutorials (`src/home/`), the in-app Application menu (`src/application-menu.mjs`), hotkey recording UI (`src/hotkey-settings.mjs`), export dialogs and image/GIF/sequence exports, Library panel UI (`src/user-library.mjs`), text dialog UI, drawing controller, stage and engine renderer, localization catalogs and the Settings pages other than recovery, interface scale and privacy.

## Documents and the engine host (DO)

Scope: how a project is created, opened, kept as a session, saved, autosaved, recovered and closed; how several projects live in one window as tabs (sessions); how the interface holds a read view of the document the engine owns; how the frame on show, thumbnails and previews are fetched from the engine. Main files: `src/editor.mjs` (the `Editor` read view, load, revision and dirty counters), `src/editor-sessions.mjs` (detach and resume of a document), `src/project-tabs.mjs` and `src/project-tabs-host.mjs` (the list and the host that moves sessions; session side of tabs only, the strip itself is `src/shell/project-tabs-view.mjs`, another area), `src/engine/*` (engine loader, open, owner, patch, shown, thumbnails, previews, pictures, composite, guard, failure, document sync), `src/pivot/*` (read helpers), `src/document-save.mjs`, `src/document-lift.mjs`, `src/lazy-payload.mjs`, and the document parts of `src/app.mjs` (`openProject`, `settleProject`, `saveNative`, `performNativeSave`, `scheduleRecovery`, `showRecovery`, `updateTitle`, `documentStateNow`, `finishDocumentAction`, `saveEveryProjectThen`) plus `src/app-dialogs.mjs` (`confirmReplace`, `chooseRecovery`, `showRecentFiles`, `rename`) and `src/native-files.mjs` / `src/browser-recovery.mjs` (transfer and storage the app code calls). Desktop counterparts the app depends on: `desktop/project-files.cjs`, `desktop/recovery.cjs`, `desktop/main.cjs` (window close question). Contracts: `docs/engine/document.md`, `docs/engine/editing.md`, `docs/engine/evaluation.md`, `docs/engine/threading.md`, `docs/engine/memory.md`, `docs/engine/rendering.md` ("In the app", "Thumbnails"), `docs/PROJECT-TABS.md`, `docs/ADAPTIVE-PREVIEW.md`, `docs/native-file-format.md`, `docs/document-exports.md`.

Native framing: the engine already owns the document, history, saving and frames in Rust; in the native app the "read view" (JS objects patched copy-on-write from session answers, lazy tables, lazy payloads) disappears and `styk-core::documents` talks to the `styk-edit` session directly. The rows below still list it, because every behaviour it provides (what is lazily read, when revisions change, when a refusal shows) must keep an owner. Test names in the "Test today" column are the files the docs and code point to; a few are inferred from naming and were not opened, treat them as leads.

| Electron IPC for documents: `activateDocument`, `selectDocument`, `releaseDocument`, `beginProjectWrite`, `writeFileChunk`, `finishFileWrite`, `cancelFileWrite`, `beginRecoveryWrite`, `beginRecoveryRead`, `listRecoveries`, `deleteRecovery`, `setDocumentState`, `onOpenFile`, `onMenuAction`, `listRecentFiles`, `openRecentFile`, `clearRecentFiles`, `listSamples`, `newWindow` | `src/app.mjs`, `src/native-files.mjs`, desktop preload | styk-platform::files; styk-platform::recovery; styk-platform::shell |

### Port notes

- Code is the truth: the autosave select is built from `presets.slice(1, -1)` in `src/settings.mjs`, so 60 s is not offered although the preset list holds it, and a stored 60 shows no extra option. The native settings must decide whether to offer 60 s (Off, 2.5, 5, 10, 30 today).
- Ctrl+W (`close-project`) is bound through the same helper as the tab keys (`projectKeys`), so it is desktop-only like them, not only Ctrl+Tab and Ctrl+PageUp/Down.
- Autosave is a recovery copy, never a write to the project file. The max-wait rule (max(interval, min(60 s, 3 x interval))) exists because every edit aborts the running snapshot; keep it or continuous editing never checkpoints. Backoff doubles per aborted attempt up to 12x and resets after a success.
- Recovery copies are keyed by the project's document token (desktop) or tab key (browser). A save or close removes only its own copy; the offer lists copies no open project owns. A restored copy is marked dirty on purpose (it is unsaved work) and adopts the copy id so the next write replaces it.
- Save happens in the original input task in the browser because the picker needs user activation; native has no such constraint, but keep "save target only after success" and "mark saved only if revision and epoch match".
- A save leaves every movieclip level first (the whole project is stored); the recovery write instead folds scopes without leaving them, so the person keeps working inside the clip.
- The `editor.dirty` setter is the only non-history way to dirty a project (rename). Native: model "metadata touch" as a revision bump without an undo entry.
- Revision numbers are global across all open documents on purpose: caches keyed by revision must never confuse two projects. Keep one monotonically increasing counter per window.
- The project picture is read from the already drawn canvas (no extra render) and only at rest; it is skipped when the view is rotated or flipped or the artboard is under 16 px. Native may render it offscreen instead, but keep the 256 px bound and the quiet-moment rule.
- Switching projects budget to keep: 82-161 ms to editable and one main-thread task per switch (58-103 ms) on the documented machine; resting projects hold no renderer, worker mirror or timers.
- Every long step of an open is sliced (4 ms) and yields; native should keep the UI thread free while a document opens, with progress and cancel.
- The 12-project limit and the busy refusal are user-visible toasts, not dialogs. A drop on a panel that handles it (library) must not also open the file.
- Thumbnails and previews are pure functions of (document revision, tick, size); stale results are dropped, never shown. `'soft'` means show and ask again.
- `src/document-normalize.mjs` is referenced only by tests (the engine normalizes on open now); `src/document-transport.mjs` is used only by the importer and old export workers; `src/zlib-sync.mjs` and most of `src/base64.mjs` serve drawing tiles (Drawing area). No native row is needed for them here.
- Some test file names in the table were inferred from the docs and glob patterns (shown with `*`); confirm before relying on them as coverage.
- Cross-references (no rows here): the tab strip widget and Ctrl+Tab switcher visuals (shell area); File menu and application menu contents (menus area); import of Stick Nodes archives and images (import area); `.piv/.stk/.stkz/.stykmov` and media exports (export area EX); clipboards (`engine-clipboards.mjs`, EX); engine stage and playback (`engine-stage.mjs`, `engine-playback.mjs`, stage and timeline areas); drawing store and workers (`engine-drawing*.mjs`, Drawing area); release notes and privacy first-run prompts queued at startup (Home and updates areas).

## Gaps found by the coverage check (GP)

Scope: features and modules the first inventory pass did not reference: `docs/FIGURE-RANGES.md`, `docs/TIMELINE-DIRECT-EDIT.md`, and seven source modules. The code was checked for each described feature. The multi-frame figure commands are already summarised in ST-062 (Edit together on the stage); the rows below add the commands and rules that row does not name. What still exists of `docs/TIMELINE-DIRECT-EDIT.md` is covered by TL-036, TL-037, TL-039, TL-047 and TL-048, so it adds no rows.

### Features and surfaces

| ID | Feature or surface | Today (code; docs) | Native owner | Test today | Screen ID | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GP-001 | Range command contract (all figure range commands): operations address figures by native `unique_id`, so a frame without that figure is skipped; every target frame is planned and validated before the first mutation; one Undo restores the whole operation; a fully motionless operation returns zero changes and records no history entry. Each returns `{changedFrames, changedTicks, insertedUids}`. Targets are keyframe indices, or explicit composed ticks (`options.ticks`, which win over indices); `options.uids` defaults to the selected figures. | `engine/crates/styk-edit/src/range.rs`, `range_plan.rs`; `src/engine/engine-commands.mjs` (command list); `docs/FIGURE-RANGES.md` | styk-edit; styk-core::timeline | `tests/editor-range-commands.test.mjs`; `tests/engine-owner-command-list.test.mjs`; Rust tests in `styk-edit` | animate.stage | todo |
| GP-002 | Add figure across the selected frames: dropping a figure asset on the stage, the Add figure action and the figure palette add one figure with one stable fresh UID in every selected frame (Edit together on). | `src/app.mjs` (`addFigureInFrames` callers); `editor.addFigureInFrames` | styk-edit; styk-core::stage | `tests/editor-range-commands.test.mjs` | animate.stage | todo |
| GP-003 | Paste figures across the selected frames: Paste with Edit together on pastes the figure clipboard into every selected frame, merging needed definitions once, keeping internal joins and using stable fresh UIDs throughout (used unless a fresh-type selection paste applies). | `src/app.mjs` (`sharedClipboard.paste('figures')`); `engine/crates/styk-edit/src/figure_clipboard.rs` | styk-edit; styk-core::clipboard | `tests/editor-range-commands.test.mjs` | animate.stage | todo |
| GP-004 | Appearance edits across frames: colour override, native transparency byte and opacity (0 to 1) set from the selection controls apply to every selected frame; with 2+ figures selected but no range the same command runs on the current frame's tick. Appearance and order edits avoid geometry evaluation. | `src/app.mjs` (`updateSelection`); `editor.updateFiguresInFrames` | styk-edit; styk-core::inspector | `tests/editor-range-commands.test.mjs` | animate.stage | todo |
| GP-005 | Draw order across frames: Bring to front / Send to back / Layer up / Layer down with Edit together on reorder in every selected frame, staying inside each figure's assigned user layer (`front`, `back` or signed steps). | `src/app.mjs` (`selection-back`, `layer-up`, `layer-down` actions); `editor.orderFiguresInFrames` | styk-edit | `tests/editor-range-commands.test.mjs` | animate.stage | todo |
| GP-006 | Rotate step across frames: the Rotate action turns the selected group by 15 degrees about its own centre in each selected frame; attached descendants rotate once and external parents stay fixed. | `src/app.mjs` (`rotate` action); `editor.rotateFiguresInFrames` | styk-edit | `tests/editor-range-commands.test.mjs` | animate.stage | todo |
| GP-007 | Align across frames: the Align buttons (axis x or y, edge start, centre or end) align the selected artwork in every selected frame using the renderer's bounds (`boundsOf`), translating complete joined components. | `src/app.mjs` (`alignSelection`); `editor.alignFiguresInFrames` | styk-edit; styk-core::stage | `tests/editor-range-commands.test.mjs` | animate.stage | todo |
| GP-008 | Scale across frames (session command with no interface caller found in `src/`): sets the reference figure's absolute scale and keeps selected proportions, or applies a multiplier with `{relative:true}`. The command is in the session list, so the native port keeps it; the inspector scale row uses the numeric edit path (IN-046). | `src/engine/engine-commands.mjs` (`scaleFiguresInFrames`); `docs/FIGURE-RANGES.md` | styk-edit | `tests/engine-owner-command-list.test.mjs` | none | todo |
| GP-009 | Range edits of authored (animated) documents: selected native rows resolve to their start ticks; explicit ticks edit the composed state; native source frames are untouched. Opacity and scale write scalar keys without pose captures; topology, presence, colour, joins and order write retained pose sources for the affected layers and update existing scalar overrides so they cannot mask the edit; unselected descendants keep independent scalar animation. Layer migration has explicit allocation limits and rejects before mutating. | `engine/crates/styk-edit/src/range_plan.rs`; `docs/FIGURE-RANGES.md` "Native and authored documents" | styk-edit; styk-doc | Rust tests in `styk-edit` | none | todo |
| GP-010 | Numeric pose values over several frames: a Root X/Y, scale, angle or draw-order value typed with several frames chosen is written as a key at every chosen tick when the edit ends (mode `absolute`); gestures show the frame on show meanwhile. The doc says this is refused; the code writes it (see "Described but removed"). | `src/native-property-edit.mjs` (`rangeCount`, `rangeTimes`, `addAnimationKeys`); `src/app.mjs` (`rangeMode: "absolute"`) | styk-core::inspector; styk-edit | `tests/native-property-session.test.mjs` | panel.inspector | todo |

### Described but removed

- Selected-channel timeline and its direct key dragging (`docs/TIMELINE-DIRECT-EDIT.md` intro): searched `src/` for a "selected channel" timeline and found none; `tests/timeline-direct-edit.browser.mjs` is now only `import './layer-timeline.browser.mjs'` ("retained command entry point for the replacement layer/filmstrip UI"). The surviving behaviour lives in the layer sheet (TL-036 to TL-039, TL-047, TL-048).
- Figure row activating a figure ID as a layer ID (the bug fix in the same doc): `src/layer-timeline.mjs` has no figure rows; its rows are layers, folders, Camera, Scene and effect/motion rows.
- Dragging "held pose blocks": no pose-block objects in `src/layer-timeline.mjs`; exposures (TL-037, TL-038) replace them.
- "Larger hit targets choose the nearest key" and "drag feedback reports replacement counts": searched `src/layer-timeline.mjs` and `src/locales/en-features` for a nearest-key search or a replacement-count message and found neither. The drag preview is dashed boxes plus a "5 -> 9" timing pill (TL-037); replacement follows the setting `replaceKeyframesOnDrop`.
- Stale line in `docs/FIGURE-RANGES.md`: "a pose value at one value across several frames is refused". `src/native-property-edit.mjs` now writes the value into every chosen frame (GP-010).
- Not removed, for the record: seeking beyond the animation end (`seek(tick,{allowBeyondEnd:true})`, limit 10,000,000 ticks in `src/editor.mjs`) and navigation without changing the exported duration (TL-036).

### Modules

- `src/editor-vfx.mjs`: one read method on the editor, `getVfxClips(target)` (clips of the report, optionally filtered by target); edits and sampling belong to the session. Covered by IN-088 (effect list rows) and IN-085 to IN-090. No new row.
- `src/engine/engine-worker.mjs`: the engine worker thread script; joins the shared memory and runs `worker_run` until the engine stops. No user-visible behaviour; covered by DO-075 (engine start-up) and DO-021 (remaining workers started early on open). Native replaces it with threads in styk-engine. No new row.
- `src/sticknodes/decoder/png-browser.mjs`: `encodePNGPortable`, a lossless RGB/RGBA PNG encoder (no canvas alpha premultiplication) used for sprite images and the legacy background during Stick Nodes import and by drawing PNG export (`src/drawing-png.mjs`). Covered by EX-067 and EX-069 and DR-067. No new row.
- `src/sticknodes/decoder/project-layout.mjs`: version-conditioned record layouts of the Stick Nodes project format (known versions 110 to 431, build up to 117) with exact field byte ranges kept for lossless pose edits; the reader behind `.stknds` opening. Covered by EX-067 and EX-069. No new row.
- `src/ui/ui-strings.mjs`: re-exports `messageText` as `kitText` and provides `translator(localization)` (accepts a localization object or a function) for the shared UI kit. Covered by SH-016 and the localization rows in `10-settings-support-l10n-touch.md`. No new row.
- `src/vfx-drawable.mjs`: `drawableVfxPadding(item)`, how far a drawn item's effects spread (own stack, segment stack, movieclip owner and nested owners), used for editor bounds and previews. Covered by IN-113 and ST-092. No new row.
- `src/vfx-paint.mjs`: `vfxPaint(value)` turns an effect colour (packed number or paint object) into a normalised solid or gradient paint. Covered by ST-085, IN-059 and IN-060. No new row.

## Appendix A: every command (CMD)

Generated from `src/ui/command-catalog.mjs` (`COMMAND_CATALOG`) and `src/hotkeys.mjs` (`HOTKEY_COMMANDS`, default bindings). One registry in `styk-core::commands` names every one of these with the same id, label key, glyph, category, default shortcut, scope, modes and availability; menus, the palette, the rail, tooltips and the automation mode pick them by id. The area sections say what each does.

## Appendix B: every desktop IPC channel (IPC)

Generated from `desktop/main.cjs`, `desktop/preload.cjs` and the services in `desktop/`. Kinds: `handle` (the main process answers an `invoke`), `on`/`listen` (one-way), `push` (main to page), `service` (named in a service module). In the native app these become direct calls to `styk-services` traits; nothing crosses a process. The DK section says what each group does.

| ID | Channel | Kinds | Preload API | Where | Native owner | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IPC-001 | `styk:audio-begin` | invoke | `beginAudioImport` | preload.cjs:133 | styk-services::audio (decode for import); styk-core::audio | todo |
| IPC-002 | `styk:audio-cancel` | invoke | `cancelAudioImport` | preload.cjs:137 | styk-services::audio (decode for import); styk-core::audio | todo |
| IPC-003 | `styk:audio-finish` | invoke | `finishAudioImport` | preload.cjs:135 | styk-services::audio (decode for import); styk-core::audio | todo |

## Appendix C: every stylesheet (CSS)

The visual contract the native widgets reproduce (sizes, spacing, colours, radii, shadows, states, transitions). `src/styles/tokens.css` is the token source (generated into `styk-design`); components and base styles become `styk-design` widgets; layout and feature styles are matched by the panels that own them. The screenshot comparison is the check.
